group, atom, negation or quantifier\n(FOLIO/MALLS convention: '∀x (A(x)) ∧ ∀y (B(y))' is two sentences).\n\"\"\"\nimport re\nimport z3\n\nTOK = re.compile(r\"\\s*(∀|∃|¬|∧|∨|→|↔|⊕|\\(|\\)|,|[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_'\\-]*)\")\nOPS = {\"↔\": \"iff\", \"⊕\": \"xor\", \"→\": \"imp\", \"∨\": \"or\", \"∧\": \"and\"}\nPREC = {\"iff\": 1, \"xor\": 1, \"imp\": 2, \"or\": 3, \"and\": 4}\n\n\ndef tokenize(s: str) -> list[str]:\n    s = s.replace(\"⇒\", \"→\").replace(\"->\", \"→\").replace(\"<->\", \"↔\").replace(\"&\", \"∧\").replace(\"|\", \"∨\")\n    s = s.replace(\"~\", \"¬\").replace(\"!\", \"¬\").replace(\"¬\", \"¬\").replace(\"−\", \"-\")\n    pos, out = 0, []\n    s = s.strip().rstrip(\".\")\n    while pos < len(s):\n        m = TOK.match(s, pos)\n        if not m:\n            raise ValueError(f\"bad char at {pos}: {s[pos:pos+10]!r}\")\n        out.append(m.group(1))\n        pos = m.end()\n        while pos < len(s) and s[pos].isspace():\n            pos += 1\n    return out\n\n\nclass Parser:\n    def __init__(self, s: str):\n        self.t = tokenize(s)\n        self.i = 0\n\n    def peek(self):\n        return self.t[self.i] if self.i < len(self.t) else None\n\n    def eat(self, x=None):\n        tok = self.peek()\n        if x is not None and tok != x:\n            raise ValueError(f\"expected {x} got {tok}\")\n        self.i += 1\n        return tok\n\n    def parse(self):\n        e = self.expr(0)\n        if self.peek() is not None:\n            raise ValueError(f\"trailing {self.t[self.i:]}\")\n        return e\n\n    def expr(self, minp):\n        lhs = self.unary()\n        while self.peek() in OPS and PREC[OPS[self.peek()]] > minp - 1 and PREC[OPS[self.peek()]] >= minp:\n            op = OPS[self.eat()]\n            # → is right-assoc, others left-assoc\n            nxt = PREC[op] if op == \"imp\" else PREC[op] + 1\n            rhs = self.expr(nxt)\n            lhs = (op, lhs, rhs)\n        return lhs\n\n    def unary(self):\n        tok = self.peek()\n        if tok == \"¬\":\n            self.eat()\n            return (\"not\", self.unary())\n        if tok in (\"∀\", \"∃\"):\n            self.eat()\n            vs = [self.eat()]\n            while self.peek() not in (\"(\", \"¬\", \"∀\", \"∃\") and self.peek() is not None and re.match(r\"^[a-z][a-z0-9]*$\", self.peek() or \"\") and not self._is_atom_start():\n                vs.append(self.eat())\n            body = self.unary()  # FOLIO/MALLS convention: a quantifier binds the next group/atom/quantifier\n            for v in reversed(vs):\n                body = (\"all\" if tok == \"∀\" else \"ex\", v, body)\n            return body\n        if tok == \"(\":\n            self.eat(\"(\")\n            e = self.expr(0)\n            self.eat(\")\")\n            return e\n        name = self.eat()\n        if self.peek() == \"(\":\n            self.eat(\"(\")\n            args = [self.eat()]\n            while self.peek() == \",\":\n                self.eat(\",\")\n                args.append(self.eat())\n            self.eat(\")\")\n            return (\"atom\", name, tuple(args))\n        return (\"atom\", name, ())  # propositional atom\n\n    def _is_atom_start(self):\n        return self.i + 1 < len(self.t) and self.t[self.i + 1] == \"(\"\n\n\ndef parse(s: str):\n    return Parser(s).parse()\n\n\ndef preds(e, acc=None):\n    acc = {} if acc is None else acc\n    if e[0] == \"atom\":\n        acc[f\"{e[1]}/{len(e[2])}\"] = len(e[2])  # key by name/arity: arity clashes are distinct symbols\n    elif e[0] in (\"all\", \"ex\"):\n        preds(e[2], acc)\n    elif e[0] == \"not\":\n        preds(e[1], acc)\n    else:\n        preds(e[1], acc)\n        preds(e[2], acc)\n    return acc\n\n\nU = z3.DeclareSort(\"U\")\n\n\ndef to_z3(e, env, P):\n    k = e[0]\n    if k in (\"all\", \"ex\"):\n        v = z3.Const(e[1], U)\n        env2 = dict(env)\n        env2[e[1]] = v\n        b = to_z3(e[2], env2, P)\n        return z3.ForAll([v], b) if k == \"all\" else z3.Exists([v], b)\n    if k == \"not\":\n        return z3.Not(to_z3(e[1], env, P))\n    if k == \"atom\":\n        args = [env.get(a, z3.Const(\"c_\" + a, U)) for a in e[2]]\n        f = P[f\"{e[1]}/{len(e[2])}\"]\n        return f(*args) if args else f\n    a, b = to_z3(e[1], env, P), to_z3(e[2], env, P)\n    return {\"and\": lambda: z3.And(a, b), \"or\": lambda: z3.Or(a, b), \"imp\": lambda: z3.Implies(a, b),\n            \"iff\": lambda: a == b, \"xor\": lambda: z3.Xor(a, b)}[k]()\n\n\ndef mkpred(name, n):\n    name = name.replace(\"/\", \"_a\")\n    return z3.Function(name, *([U] * n), z3.BoolSort()) if n else z3.Bool(name)\n\n\ndef valid(c, ms=3000):\n    s = z3.Solver()\n    s.set(\"timeout\", ms)\n    s.add(z3.Not(c))\n    r = s.check()\n    return True if r == z3.unsat else (False if r == z3.sat else None)\n\n\ndef profile(e, ms=3000):\n    \"\"\"Semantic monotonicity of formula e in each predicate: UP / DOWN / VACUOUS / NONMONO / UNKNOWN.\"\"\"\n    ar = preds(e)\n    base = {q: mkpred(q, m) for q, m in ar.items()}\n    out = {}\n    for p, n in ar.items():\n        alt = dict(base)\n        alt[p] = mkpred(p + \"_prime\", n)\n        xs = [z3.Const(f\"_x{i}\", U) for i in range(n)]\n        sub = z3.ForAll(xs, z3.Implies(base[p](*xs), alt[p](*xs))) if n else z3.Implies(base[p], alt[p])\n        F, Fp = to_z3(e, {}, base), to_z3(e, {}, alt)\n        up, down = valid(z3.Implies(z3.And(sub, F), Fp), ms), valid(z3.Implies(z3.And(sub, Fp), F), ms)\n        if up is None or down is None:\n            out[p] = \"UNKNOWN\"\n        else:\n            out[p] = {(True, True): \"VACUOUS\", (True, False): \"UP\", (False, True): \"DOWN\", (False, False): \"NONMONO\"}[(up, down)]\n    return out\n\n\ndef equivalent(e1, e2, ms=5000):\n    ar = {**preds(e1), **preds(e2)}\n    P = {q: mkpred(q, m) for q, m in ar.items()}\n    return valid(to_z3(e1, {}, P) == to_z3(e2, {}, P), ms)\n\n\ndef connectivity_flags(e):\n    \"\"\"Binding check: inside each quantifier scope, variables must be linked through shared atoms.", "numLines": 175, "startLine": 1, "totalLines": 262}}

exchange\n            yield \"MOVE\", replace(e, path, (\"imp\", (\"and\", node[1], node[2][2]), node[2][1]))\n        if k in BIN:\n            if k == \"imp\":\n                yield \"REV\", replace(e, path, (\"imp\", node[2], node[1]))\n            for op in BIN:\n                if op != k:\n                    lab = \"RESTR\" if {k, op} == {\"imp\", \"and\"} and parent_kind(e, path) in (\"all\", \"ex\") else \"CONN\"\n                    yield lab, replace(e, path, (op, node[1], node[2]))\n            if k in (\"and\", \"or\"):\n                yield \"DROP\", replace(e, path, node[1])\n                yield \"DROP\", replace(e, path, node[2])\n        if k == \"atom\":\n            args = node[2]\n            if len(args) >= 2:\n                for perm in set(itertools.permutations(args)):\n                    if perm != args:\n                        yield \"SWAP\", replace(e, path, (\"atom\", node[1], perm))\n            pool = set(scope) | Ct\n            for i, a in enumerate(args):\n                for b in pool:\n                    if b != a:\n                        yield \"BIND\", replace(e, path, (\"atom\", node[1], args[:i] + (b,) + args[i + 1:]))\n        # ADD: conjoin a corrected-only atom (args re-bound to in-scope variables when its variables are foreign)\n        if k != \"atom\" or True:\n            for a in new_atoms:\n                sc = list(scope)\n                opts = []\n                for x in a[2]:\n                    opts.append([x] if (x in Ct or x in sc) else (sc or [x]))\n                for combo in itertools.islice(itertools.product(*opts), 6):\n                    yield \"ADD\", replace(e, path, (\"and\", node, (\"atom\", a[1], tuple(combo))))\n\n\ndef search(ao, target, budget_s=25.0, max_d2=6000):\n    ft = fp(target)\n    t0 = time.time()\n    lvl1 = []\n    seen = set()\n    for lab, c in edits(ao, target):\n        s = str(c)\n        if s in seen:\n            continue\n        seen.add(s)\n        lvl1.append((lab, c))\n        if fp(c) == ft and equivalent(c, target, ms=1500) is True:\n            return [lab], time.time() - t0\n    n = 0\n    for lab1, c1 in lvl1:\n        for lab2, c2 in edits(c1, target):\n            n += 1\n            if lab1 == \"NEG\" and lab2 == \"NEG\":\n                continue\n            if n > max_d2 * 10 or time.time() - t0 > budget_s:\n                return None, time.time() - t0\n            if fp(c2) == ft and equivalent(c2, target, ms=1500) is True:\n                return sorted([lab1, lab2]), time.time() - t0\n    return None, time.time() - t0\n\n\ndef load(p):\n    return [json.loads(l) for l in open(p) if l.strip()]\n\n\ndef pairs():\n    for r in load(D / \"DSAVlab-UNIUD_MALLS_test_subset-CURATED__MALLS_instances.jsonl\"):\n        yield \"MALLS\", r[\"id\"], r[\"NL_sentence\"], r[\"FOL_sentence_old\"], r[\"FOL_sentence_new\"], str(r.get(\"ambiguity\", \"NA\"))\n    for r in load(D / \"DSAVlab-UNIUD_FOLIO_validation-curated__FOLIO_instances.jsonl\"):\n        if str(r[\"id\"]).startswith(\"story\"):\n            continue\n        yield \"FOLIO-concl\", r[\"id\"], r[\"NL_sentence\"], r[\"FOL_sentence_old\"], r[\"FOL_sentence\"], str(r.get(\"ambiguity\", \"NA\"))\n\n\ndef work(item):\n    src, iid, nl, old, new, amb = item\n    try:\n        oe, ne = parse(old), parse(new)\n    except Exception:\n        return None\n    if equivalent(oe, ne) is True:\n        return None\n    ao, pmap, cmap = align(oe, ne)\n    rec = {\"src\": src, \"id\": iid, \"nl\": nl, \"old\": old, \"new\": new, \"ambiguous\": amb == \"True\",\n           \"pmap\": {f\"{k[0]}/{k[1]}\": v for k, v in pmap.items()}, \"cmap\": cmap}\n    if equivalent(ao, ne) is True:\n        rec[\"cls\"] = \"VOCAB\"; return rec\n    gb = gran_bridge(ao, ne)\n    if gb and equivalent(gb[0], gb[1]) is True:\n        rec[\"cls\"] = \"GRAN\"; return rec\n    base, tgt = (gb if gb else (ao, ne))\n    ops, dt = search(base, tgt)\n    rec[\"secs\"] = round(dt, 1)\n    rec[\"cls\"] = \"+\".join(ops) if ops else \"COMPOUND\"\n    rec[\"depth\"] = len(ops) if ops else None\n    return rec\n\n\ndef main():\n    from multiprocessing import Pool\n    items = list(pairs())\n    with Pool(2) as pool:\n        recs = [r for r in pool.imap_unordered(work, items, chunksize=1) if r]\n    out = Path(__file__).with_suffix(\".rows.json\")\n    out.write_text(json.dumps(recs, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1))\n    n = len(recs)\n    c = Counter(r[\"cls\"] for r in recs)\n    print(f\"non-equivalent pairs: {n}\")\n    for k, v in c.most_common():\n        print(f\"  {k:22s} {v:3d}  ({v/n:.2f})\")\n    vocab = sum(r[\"cls\"] in (\"VOCAB\", \"GRAN\") for r in recs)\n    err = [r for r in recs if r[\"cls\"] not in (\"VOCAB\", \"GRAN\")]\n    single = sum(r.get(\"depth\") == 1 for r in err)\n    two = sum(r.get(\"depth\") == 2 for r in err)\n    comp = sum(r[\"cls\"] == \"COMPOUND\" for r in err)\n    print(f\"vocabulary/granularity-only (auto): {vocab}/{n}\")\n    print(f\"remaining errors: {len(err)}  single-op: {single}  two-op: {two}  compound(>2 or unresolved): {comp}\")\n    ops = Counter(o for r in err if r.get(\"depth\") for o in r[\"cls\"].split(\"+\"))\n    print(\"operator mass among repaired errors:\", dict(ops.most_common()))\n    for amb in (True, False):\n        sub = [r for r in recs if r[\"ambiguous\"] == amb]\n        print(f\"ambiguous={amb}: n={len(sub)}\", dict(Counter(r[\"cls\"] for r in sub).most_common()))\n    for s in (\"MALLS\", \"FOLIO-concl\"):\n        sub = [r for r in recs if r[\"src\"] == s]\n        print(f\"{s}: n={len(sub)}\", dict(Counter(r[\"cls\"] for r in sub).most_common()))\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.setrecursionlimit(10000)\n    main()\n", "numLines": 260, "startLine": 200, "totalLines": 459}}

  "summary": "Screens two verbalise-and-entail candidates for gold-free NL->FOL faithfulness on the run's SHARED frozen screen: Logic-LM's released FOLIO-dev outputs from 3 LLMs (track L, primary) plus 302 curated FOLIO/MALLS annotator items (track H, secondary), with byte-identical item_ids and labels across the sibling experiments. B1 renders the candidate into literal, non-repairing template English and scores bidirectional NLI (DeBERTa-v3-large MNLI/FEVER/ANLI/LING/WANLI, plus 2 robustness checkpoints). B2 generates the candidate's own typed single-edit mutants (census operators), asks z3 for tiny finite worlds that separate candidate from mutant in both directions, verbalises each world, and has a TEXT-ONLY judge (NLI, then a cheap OpenRouter LLM) say whether the SENTENCE is TRUE / FALSE / UNDETERMINED there. Scores: mismatch rate against the candidate's own truth value in each world, and the UNDETERMINED rate, which is pre-registered as the dropped-condition signal (the world only mentions the candidate's predicates, so a condition the candidate lost leaves the sentence undecidable). Everything is pre-registered in prereg.json before track-L labels are read; operating thresholds are calibrated on track-H corrected gold only. Deliverables: method_out.json with per-item scores keyed by the shared item_id, AUROC/AUPRC with sentence-clustered paired bootstrap CIs, tie-aware pairwise accuracy, per-census-type sensitivity, rewrite false alarms per family, coverage with failures in the denominator, cost per item, the world-judging gate table by length stratum, contamination deltas (original vs nonce-disguised), and reusable functions verbalize(fol), b1_score(text, fol), build_worlds(fol), b2_score(text, fol). NAACL 2022; FRANK, Pagnoni et al. 2021; Goyal & Durrett 2021; Deutsch, Foster & Freitag EMNLP 2023 'Ties Matter'); the Logic-LLM repo (outputs/logic_programs/FOLIO_dev_{gpt-3.5-turbo,gpt-4,text-davinci-003}.json; records have keys id, context, question, answer, options, raw_logic_programs, where each program is a text block with 'Predicates:', 'Premises:' and 'Conclusion:' sections of 'FOL ::: NL' lines, and the Predicates section carries a system-written English gloss per predicate); the MoritzLaurer/DeBERTa-v3-large-mnli-fever-anli-ling-wanli card (labels entailment/neutral/contradiction, MNLI 91.2/90.8, ANLI-all 70.2, ANLI-R3 64.0); the tasksource NLI cards (deberta-base-long-nli / deberta-v3-large-tasksource-nli are TRAINED ON FOLIO, FOL-nli and LogicNLI, i.e. contaminated for this screen); Raza & Milic-Frayling IJCAI 2025 (arXiv 2501.16961, Semantic Self-Verification: the LLM generates concrete instantiations of the problem, the solver checks the formalisation against them, with near-perfect precision over part of the cases). HOW THIS KIND OF STUDY IS BUILT. (1) BASELINES every reference-free faithfulness paper reports: an LLM judge (cheap and strong), NLI-based entailment scores (SummaC/TRUE-style: NLI between source and output, taken in both directions for coverage), a QA/probe-based metric, round-trip similarity, and trivial baselines (length, parse/compile rate). For formal outputs the field adds solver checks such as round-trip equivalence (2604.25031) and sampling consensus (2410.20936). The first comparison a reviewer names for an NLI-on-verbalisation metric is round-trip with an LLM verbaliser (sibling D computes it) and the plain LLM judge. The fair-tuning convention is one fixed prompt per model with no tuning on the evaluation labels, and thresholds fixed on a separate calibration set. (2) DATA: FOLIO and MALLS are the standard sentence-level NL->FOL corpora, usable only with the 2606.02837 corrected gold. Logic-LM's released outputs are the standard real-system outputs on FOLIO. ProverQA is template-generated and has no long conditioned sentences (iter-3 probe: 0/8,948). (3) CONTROLS: the same items and labels for every metric (paired design); the NLI checkpoint and prompt fixed before labels are seen; contamination checked with disguised or renamed items; invariance checked with meaning-preserving rewrites. (4) HOW MUCH IS ENOUGH: TRUE/SummaC-style meta-evaluations report ROC AUC on a few hundred to a few thousand binary-labelled items, with bootstrap CIs. Below about 50 positives and 50 negatives per cell nobody reads a per-stratum AUROC as more than descriptive. With about 150 errors and 350 correct items, the AUROC SE is about 0.025 and the minimum detectable paired ΔAUROC is about 0.06-0.07. (5) MEASURES: item-level ROC AUC (threshold-free) is the primary measure, with AUPRC always stated alongside its prevalence. Precision/recall/false-alarm rate are reported at a threshold fixed in advance. Binary or quantised scores are compared with tie-aware pairwise accuracy (Deutsch et al. 2023). Uncertainty comes from a paired bootstrap resampled by sentence cluster. Per-error-type sensitivity is reported in the FRANK tradition, and cost in $ and seconds per item.",
  "practice_alignment": "MEETS: (a) real system outputs as the primary evidence (Logic-LM, 3 LLMs) with corrected or double-annotated references, never original FOLIO gold alone; (b) item-level AUROC primary, AUPRC with prevalence, sentence-clustered paired bootstrap (2,000 resamples), tie-aware pairwise accuracy for the quantised B2 scores; (c) thresholds fixed on a calibration set disjoint from the selection measure (track-H corrected gold), plus prereg.json written before track-L labels are read; (d) bidirectional NLI in the SummaC/TRUE tradition, with the NLI checkpoint named and a second, uncontaminated checkpoint as a robustness check; (e) contamination control via nonce-disguised items, and an explicit flag that tasksource NLI models were trained on FOLIO; (f) invariance under 5 STRICT rewrite families; (g) coverage failures kept in the denominator and scored as flagged; (h) per-type sensitivity on the census classes (FRANK-style); (i) cost in $ and GPU/CPU seconds per item. DEPARTURES. (1) No LLM-judge or round-trip baseline is computed IN this artifact. Sibling experiment D computes them on identical item_ids, and iteration 2's evaluation joins them for the ΔAUROC-over-judge rule. Cost: this artifact alone cannot claim incremental value over the judge. It reports only incremental value over the local cheap baselines it can run (bag-of-words content accounting and the L1 lint, both reused from iter-3 code) and over each other (B2 over B1). (2) Labels are automatic (modulo-vocabulary equivalence plus depth-2 typed repair), not panel-adjudicated, per the strategy's wide-screen trade. The dataset artifact adjudicates in parallel. Cost: aligner over-acceptance (e.g. Sugar->MadeFromSugar) can place real errors in CORRECT, and B1/B2, being text-aware, are the metrics most likely to flag exactly those, which would make them look falsely noisy. Mitigation: label_fine is emitted so that iteration 2 can re-score, and the GRAN-bridge cases are held out as UNCERTAIN in the primary analysis. (3) The long, heavily conditioned stratum is untestable here (FOLIO has about 19 such premises); it is reported descriptively and the claim is deferred to the held-out set. (4) Screen depth is one template set, one prompt and no hyperparameter search. A narrow loss (<0.03 AUROC) is carried forward, not killed. (5) Power: track L will probably hold 100-200 errors, which makes the per-stratum and per-system cells underpowered. The plan therefore pre-declares the testable cells (>=50/50) and labels the rest descriptive, rather than adding more metric variants. (6) B2 overlaps with SSV (Raza & Milic-Frayling 2025) in the solver+instantiation idea, but the direction is reversed: the solver makes the worlds and a text-only judge evaluates them, where SSV has the LLM make instances and the solver check them.   "builds_on": "REUSES FROM THE RUN (all read-only; copy into the workspace, never edit in place).   "implementation_pseudocode": "# ===== 0. SETUP (gpu_basic; Python 3.12; uv) =====\n# uv pip install z3-solver torch transformers accelerate sentencepiece protobuf nltk spacy lemminflect scikit-learn numpy pandas aiohttp loguru tqdm\n# python -m spacy download en_core_web_sm ; nltk.download('wordnet'); nltk.download('omw-1.4')\n# Read skills aii-python, aii-parallel-computing, aii-use-hardware, aii-long-running-tasks, aii-openrouter-llms, aii-json before coding.\n# Layout: src/{fol.py, repair_census.py, lint_smells.py, content_accounting.py (copied), screen.py, verbalize.py, worlds.py, nli.py, llm_judge.py, disguise.py, rewrites.py, analysis.py, b_metrics.py (public API)}, data/, out/, logs/, prereg.json, method_out.json\n# Global: seed 0; every sampling ordered by sha1; cost ledger out/cost_ledger.jsonl appended after EVERY OpenRouter call; HARD_STOP_USD = 9.0.\n\n# ===== 1. SHARED SCREEN (identical to siblings A/C/D/E) =====\ncopy iter_3 fol.py, repair_census.py; patch PREC xor->3; unit-test parse('A(x) ⊕ B(x) → C(x)') == ('imp', ('xor',A,B), C)\ndef norm(t): lowercase, collapse whitespace, strip punctuation incl. trailing '.', unify quotes\ndownload Logic-LM FOLIO_dev_{gpt-3.5-turbo,gpt-4,text-davinci-003}.json (raw.githubusercontent.com/teacherpeterpan/Logic-LLM/main/outputs/logic_programs/...)\nfor each record r, prog in r['raw_logic_programs'][0]: split sections by 'Premises:' / 'Conclusion:'; each line 'FOL ::: NL' -> (nl, fol) with role premise|conclusion  # IGNORE the 'Predicates:' glosses for the main metrics (not available in the user's operating situation); keep them in a side file for the B1-gloss diagnostic only\ncurated_concl = {norm(NL_sentence): row for DSAVlab FOLIO rows whose id does not start with 'story'}  # reference = FOL_sentence (corrected)\nagreed_premises = for each story row: split NL_sentence / FOL_sentence_old lines; find the same story in folio_refined_validation.csv (match on the norm of the first premise); pair lines by norm(nl); keep a premise if parse OK on both and equivalent_modulo_vocab(orig, refined) is True -> reference = the original-gold line\nTRACK L: every Logic-LM (system, nl, fol) with norm(nl) in curated_concl (reference = corrected gold)\n   log match_rate = distinct matched conclusions / total Logic-LM conclusions\n   if distinct matched conclusions < 150: ALSO add Logic-LM premises of matched stories with norm(nl) in agreed_premises (the ONLY allowed extension); else premises are still added (the strategy also admits premise items; record ref_type)\nTRACK H: 302 curated items (202 FOLIO conclusions + 100 MALLS): candidate = FOL_sentence_old, reference = corrected; ambiguity True -> label READING_CHOICE\nitem_id = sha1(system + '|' + norm(text) + '|' + candidate_fol)[:16]  (system = 'gpt-4' etc. for L; 'human_FOLIO'/'human_MALLS' for H)\ndef equivalent_modulo_vocab(c, r): ao,_,_ = align(c, r); if equivalent(ao, r) is True -> 'VOCAB'; gb = gran_bridge(ao, r); if gb and equivalent(*gb) is True -> 'GRAN'; else False (None on timeout)\ndef label(cand_str, ref_str):\n   try parse both except -> 'UNPARSEABLE' (a candidate parse failure; a reference parse failure -> drop the item and log it)\n   eq = equivalent(c, r, 5000): True -> fine='EXACT', coarse='CORRECT'; None -> 'TIMEOUT_UNKNOWN'\n   v = equivalent_modulo_vocab: 'VOCAB' -> CORRECT; 'GRAN' -> UNCERTAIN (fine GRAN)\n   ops = search(base, tgt, budget_s=25): ops -> ERROR (fine 'ERR:'+'+'.join(ops)); None -> UNCERTAIN (fine COMPOUND)\n   # emit BOTH label (coarse) and label_fine so that iteration 2 can re-map if siblings resolved 'VOCAB/GRAN-borderline' differently\nstrata(text, ref_fol): words (<12 / 12-19 / >=20), n_quant, nesting depth (max AST depth of connectives), n_conditions (number of conjuncts in antecedents/restrictors of the reference), exception marker (regex unless|except|excluding|other than|but not|without)\nINVARIANCE SET: 150 track-L CORRECT items, first by item_id sort; STRICT rewrites (seed 0): RENAME (each predicate -> WordNet synonym of its head token of equal arity, or an '_x' suffix if none; constants -> case/underscore variant), REORDER (swap operands of every ∧/∨), DEMORGAN (push one ¬ through ∧/∨ or introduce ¬¬-free De Morgan at the top connective), PRENEX (pull quantifiers out where no capture occurs), CONTRAPOS (A→B => ¬B→¬A at the top implication); z3-verify each non-rename rewrite as equivalent to the original; drop and count failures\nwrite data/screen_items.json [{item_id, track, system, text, candidate_fol, reference_fol, ref_type, label, label_fine, repair_ops, ambiguous, strata, story_id}] + sha256 of the label vector in the log\n\n# ===== 2. PRE-REGISTRATION (before any score is joined to track-L labels) =====\nprereg.json = {timestamp, sha256 of src/*.py, verbalizer templates, NLI model ids+revisions, LLM model id + exact prompt, mutant operator list + sampling rule, world limits, score definitions + orientation (higher = more likely error), operating thresholds = the 90th percentile of each score on track-H items labelled CORRECT (known-good human gold; NOT track L), selection-relevant analyses, stratum testability rule (>=50 ERROR and >=50 CORRECT), bootstrap settings}\n\n# ===== 3. B1: template verbaliser + bidirectional NLI =====\ndef pred_phrase(name, args):  # deterministic, non-repairing\n   toks = split camelCase/snake/digits -> lower\n   head = toks[0]\n   if head is a verb per WordNet (any verb synset) and not (noun-first and no verb morphology) and not ending in -ed/-ing: VERB -> '{a0} ' + lemminflect 3sg(head) + rest + (' ' + a1 ... joined with ' and ' for arity>2)\n   elif head endswith ('ed','ing') or WordNet adj/participle: '{a0} is ' + ' '.join(toks) + (' ' + a1)\n   else NOUN: unary '{a0} is a/an ' + phrase ; binary '{a0} is ' + phrase + ' of ' + a1 ; arity>=3 '{a0}, {a1} and {a2} stand in the ' + phrase + ' relation'\n   args: variables stay as 'x','y'; constants -> split camel/underscore, Title Case\n   arity 0: 'it is the case that ' + phrase\ndef verbalize(e):\n   all v B  -> 'for every thing ' + v + ', ' + verbalize(B)\n   ex v B   -> 'there is some thing ' + v + ' such that ' + verbalize(B)\n   not atom -> atom phrase with 'is not'/'does not' ; not complex -> 'it is not the case that (' + ... + ')'\n   imp -> 'if ' + A + ', then ' + B ; iff -> A + ' if and only if ' + B ; xor -> 'either ' + A + ' or ' + B + ', but not both' ; or -> A + ' or ' + B ; and -> A + ' and ' + B\n   nested binaries parenthesised with commas only where a child is itself binary (to keep scope visible); NO simplification, NO restrictor repair: ∃x(A→B) -> 'there is some thing x such that if x is an A, then x is a B'\n   capitalise the first letter, add '.'\nNLI models: M1 = MoritzLaurer/DeBERTa-v3-large-mnli-fever-anli-ling-wanli (primary); M2 = cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-base (robustness, MNLI+SNLI, NOT FOLIO-trained); M3 = tasksource/deberta-base-long-nli (FOLIO-TRAINED: contamination probe only). Read label indices from model.config.id2label (never hard-code); fp16; batch 32 (halve on OOM); max_length 512 (M3: 1280); log the truncation count\nb1_t2f = P_entail(premise=text, hyp=verb) ; b1_f2t = P_entail(premise=verb, hyp=text) ; b1_min = min ; b1_contra = max(P_contra both ways)\noriented error scores: 1-b1_min, 1-b1_t2f (predicted DROP detector), 1-b1_f2t (predicted ADD detector), b1_contra\nB1-gloss DIAGNOSTIC (track L only, clearly marked not-deployable): the same, but predicates rendered with Logic-LM's own 'Predicates:' gloss; it measures how much of B1's failure is predicate-name terseness\n\n# ===== 4. B2: solver-built distinguishing worlds judged against the TEXT =====\nMUT_OPS = {NEG, REV, QUANT, RESTR, CONN, DROP, SWAP, SCOPE, MOVE, UNGLUE, BIND}  # ADD needs vocabulary outside φ; its role is played by DROP-mutant worlds (they vary an added conjunct) and by the UNDETERMINED signal\ndef mutants(φ): cands = [(op,ψ) for op,ψ in edits(φ, φ) if op in MUT_OPS]; dedupe by str; drop ψ with fp(ψ)==fp(φ) and equivalent(ψ,φ,1500) is True (also drop None/timeouts and count them)\n   order by sha1(item_id|op|str(ψ)); round-robin over operator types; keep K<=10 (at least 6 when available)\ndef world(φ, ψ, want_phi_true):\n   consts = constants of φ∪ψ ; for k_extra in 0..3 (total <= max(4, len(consts)+1), hard cap 6):\n      D = z3.EnumSort('D', consts_names + ['thing1'..'thing{k}'])   # constants = distinct named elements (unique-names assumption)\n      translate φ, ψ with a copy of fol.to_z3 parameterised by sort D (atoms over constants map to their enum element)\n      goal = And(φ, Not(ψ)) if want_phi_true else And(Not(φ), ψ)\n      opt = z3.Optimize(); opt.set(timeout=2000); opt.add(goal); opt.minimize(Sum(If(atom,1,0) for every ground atom))  # the smallest, most readable world\n      if sat: return facts = {ground atom: bool}   # fall back to Solver() when Optimize is unknown\n   return None\nfor each (op,ψ): W+ = world(φ,ψ,True) (φ TRUE there), W- = world(φ,ψ,False) (φ FALSE); dedupe worlds by fact set; verify each world by a pure-Python evaluator of φ (fol AST over the finite domain) and assert that truth == the expected value\ndef verbalize_world(facts): 'There are exactly N individuals: Bonnie, thing1 and thing2.' + for each unary/0-ary predicate and each individual, one positive or negative fact sentence via pred_phrase ('Bonnie is a student.' / 'thing1 is not a student.'); for binary+: the positive facts, then 'No other <phrase> facts hold.'; cap 60 facts, and if exceeded mark the world 'too_big' and skip it\nJUDGE-NLI: premise = situation, hypothesis = text -> argmax label: entailment=TRUE, contradiction=FALSE, neutral=UNDETERMINED; keep the probabilities\nJUDGE-LLM (OpenRouter; primary cheap model chosen with aii-openrouter-llms at <= $0.15/M input, e.g. google/gemini-2.5-flash-lite or its current successor; reasoning OFF; temperature 0): ONE call per item with up to 20 numbered situations; the prompt shows ONLY the sentence and the situations (never a formula) and asks, for each situation, TRUE / FALSE / UNDETERMINED ('the situation does not say enough to decide'), returning strict JSON {\"1\":\"TRUE\",...}; parse, retry once on invalid JSON, else mark that item's LLM judge a coverage failure\n   ROBUSTNESS LLM: a second family (e.g. qwen/qwen3-30b-a3b-instruct or meta-llama/llama-3.3-70b-instruct) on a 300-item sha1 subsample of track L\n   BATCHING CHECK: 100 items re-judged one world per call; report the agreement with batched verdicts\nscores per item (for each judge j ∈ {nli_M1, nli_M2, llm, llm2}):\n   n_worlds; determined = worlds judged TRUE/FALSE\n   b2_mismatch_j = (# determined worlds with judge truth != φ truth) / max(1, #determined)\n   b2_undet_j = (# UNDETERMINED) / n_worlds   # pre-registered DROP/coverage signal\n   b2_score_j = b2_mismatch_j * (1 - b2_undet_j) + 0.5 * b2_undet_j   # pre-registered combination: undetermined counts half\n   also per-direction mismatch (W+ vs W-) and per-operator mismatch, used as the error-type readout: the operator whose worlds disagree most = predicted type\ncoverage_status: 'ok' | 'unparseable' | 'no_mutants' | 'no_world' (every world build failed or timed out) | 'too_big' | 'judge_fail'; for AUROC, every non-ok item gets the MAX error score (flagged), and coverage is also reported separately\nGATE (world-judging accuracy): run the same B2 pipeline with the REFERENCE formula ρ in place of φ, on track-H corrected gold (302) + the distinct track-L references; truth of the sentence in each world := ρ's truth (exact); accuracy_j = fraction of worlds where the judge answers exactly that truth (UNDETERMINED counts as wrong; its rate is reported separately); table by words stratum × judge × operator × direction; target >= 0.85; if NLI accuracy < 0.70 on >=20-word sentences, record it and declare b2_score_llm the PRIMARY B2 score (this rule is written in prereg.json)\n\n# ===== 5. SECONDARY TRACKS =====\nSYNTH (for the C3 MIX term; never pooled with real-error AUROC): for each track-H corrected gold formula and each census operator applicable (NEG, REV, QUANT, RESTR, CONN, DROP, SWAP, SCOPE, ADD-of-a-foreign-atom drawn from another item of the same story), one sha1-chosen perturbation that is z3-non-equivalent to the reference; score B1 and B2(NLI + LLM); per-operator synthetic sensitivity at the pre-registered threshold\nDISGUISE (contamination): disguise(text, fol) = sha1-seeded consistent map from each content lemma (spaCy lemma, POS NOUN/VERB/ADJ/PROPN) that matches a predicate/constant token (by lemma or WordNet derivation) to a pronounceable nonce word (CVCV+CVC from a fixed syllable table); replace all inflections in the text and the matching token inside the predicate/constant names; function words, quantifier words, 'not/if/unless' untouched; assert that the mapping is consistent on all items (a text word maps to one nonce everywhere); run B1 (M1, M3) and B2 (NLI M1 + LLM) on disguised track-L + track-H; contamination delta = AUROC(orig) - AUROC(disguised), paired bootstrap; expect M3 (FOLIO-trained) to show the largest delta\nINVARIANCE: score B1/B2 on the 150×5 rewrites; FA_family = fraction flagged at the pre-registered threshold; also flip_rate = flagged(rewrite) & not flagged(original)\nLOCAL BASELINES on identical items (reuse iter-3 code): L1 lint (any smell → 1), bag-of-words content accounting (content_accounting.py score), parse_ok, and formula length (#atoms) as a trivial control\n\n# ===== 6. z3-verify each non-rename rewrite as equivalent to the original; drop and count failures\\nwrite data/screen_items.json [{item_id, track, system, text, candidate_fol, reference_fol, ref_type, label, label_fine, repair_ops, ambiguous, strata, story_id}] + sha256 of the label vector in the log\\n\\n# ===== 2. PRE-REGISTRATION (before any score is joined to track-L labels) =====\\nprereg.json = {timestamp, sha256 of src/*.py, verbalizer templates, NLI model ids+revisions, LLM model id + exact prompt, mutant operator list + sampling rule, world limits, score definitions + orientation (higher = more likely error), operating thresholds = the 90th percentile of each score on track-H items labelled CORRECT (known-good human gold; NOT track L), selection-relevant analyses, stratum testability rule (>=50 ERROR and >=50 CORRECT), bootstrap settings}\\n\\n# ===== 3. B1: template verbaliser + bidirectional NLI =====\\ndef pred_phrase(name, args):  # deterministic, non-repairing\\n   toks = split camelCase/snake/digits -> lower\\n   head = toks[0]\\n   if head is a verb per WordNet (any verb synset) and not (noun-first and no verb morphology) and not ending in -ed/-ing: VERB -> '{a0} ' + lemminflect 3sg(head) + rest + (' ' + a1 ... joined with ' and ' for arity>2)\\n   elif head endswith ('ed','ing') or WordNet adj/participle: '{a0} is ' + ' '.join(toks) + (' ' + a1)\\n   else NOUN: unary '{a0} is a/an ' + phrase ; binary '{a0} is ' + phrase + ' of ' + a1 ; arity>=3 '{a0}, {a1} and {a2} stand in the ' + phrase + ' relation'\\n   args: variables stay as 'x','y'; constants -> split camel/underscore, Title Case\\n   arity 0: 'it is the case that ' + phrase\\ndef verbalize(e):\\n   all v B  -> 'for every thing ' + v + ', ' + verbalize(B)\\n   ex v B   -> 'there is some thing ' + v + ' such that ' + verbalize(B)\\n   not atom -> atom phrase with 'is not'/'does not' ; not complex -> 'it is not the case that (' + ... + ')'\\n   imp -> 'if ' + A + ', then ' + B ; iff -> A + ' if and only if ' + B ; xor -> 'either ' + A + ' or ' + B + ', but not both' ; or -> A + ' or ' + B ; and -> A + ' and ' + B\\n   nested binaries parenthesised with commas only where a child is itself binary (to keep scope visible); NO simplification, NO restrictor repair: ∃x(A→B) -> 'there is some thing x such that if x is an A, then x is a B'\\n   capitalise the first letter, add '.'\\nNLI models: M1 = MoritzLaurer/DeBERTa-v3-large-mnli-fever-anli-ling-wanli (primary); M2 = cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-base (robustness, MNLI+SNLI, NOT FOLIO-trained); M3 = tasksource/deberta-base-long-nli (FOLIO-TRAINED: contamination probe only). Read label indices from model.config.id2label (never hard-code); fp16; batch 32 (halve on OOM); max_length 512 (M3: 1280); log the truncation count\\nb1_t2f = P_entail(premise=text, hyp=verb) ; b1_f2t = P_entail(premise=verb, hyp=text) ; b1_min = min ; b1_contra = max(P_contra both ways)\\noriented error scores: 1-b1_min, 1-b1_t2f (predicted DROP detector), 1-b1_f2t (predicted ADD detector), b1_contra\\nB1-gloss DIAGNOSTIC (track L only, clearly marked not-deployable): the same, but predicates rendered with Logic-LM's own 'Predicates:' gloss; it measures how much of B1's failure is predicate-name terseness\\n\\n# ===== 4. B2: solver-built distinguishing worlds judged against the TEXT =====\\nMUT_OPS = {NEG, REV, QUANT, RESTR, CONN, DROP, SWAP, SCOPE, MOVE, UNGLUE, BIND}  # ADD needs vocabulary outside φ; its role is played by DROP-mutant worlds (they vary an added conjunct) and by the UNDETERMINED signal\\ndef mutants(φ): cands = [(op,ψ) for op,ψ in edits(φ, φ) if op in MUT_OPS]; dedupe by str; drop ψ with fp(ψ)==fp(φ) and equivalent(ψ,φ,1500) is True (also drop None/timeouts and count them)\\n   order by sha1(item_id|op|str(ψ)); round-robin over operator types; keep K<=10 (at least 6 when available)\\ndef world(φ, ψ, want_phi_true):\\n   consts = constants of φ∪ψ ; for k_extra in 0..3 (total <= max(4, len(consts)+1), hard cap 6):\\n      D = z3.EnumSort('D', consts_names + ['thing1'..'thing{k}'])   # constants = distinct named elements (unique-names assumption)\\n      translate φ, ψ with a copy of fol.to_z3 parameterised by sort D (atoms over constants map to their enum element)\\n      goal = And(φ, Not(ψ)) if want_phi_true else And(Not(φ), ψ)\\n      opt = z3.Optimize(); opt.set(timeout=2000); opt.add(goal); opt.minimize(Sum(If(atom,1,0) for every ground atom))  # the smallest, most readable world\\n      if sat: return facts = {ground atom: bool}   # fall back to Solver() when Optimize is unknown\\n   return None\\nfor each (op,ψ): W+ = world(φ,ψ,True) (φ TRUE there), W- = world(φ,ψ,False) (φ FALSE); dedupe worlds by fact set; verify each world by a pure-Python evaluator of φ (fol AST over the finite domain) and assert that truth == the expected value\\ndef verbalize_world(facts): 'There are exactly N individuals: Bonnie, thing1 and thing2.' + for each unary/0-ary predicate and each individual, one positive or negative fact sentence via pred_phrase ('Bonnie is a student.' / 'thing1 is not a student.'); for binary+: the positive facts, then 'No other <phrase> facts hold.'; cap 60 facts, and if exceeded mark the world 'too_big' and skip it\\nJUDGE-NLI: premise = situation, hypothesis = text -> argmax label: entailment=TRUE, contradiction=FALSE, neutral=UNDETERMINED; keep the probabilities\\nJUDGE-LLM (OpenRouter; primary cheap model chosen with aii-openrouter-llms at <= $0.15/M input, e.g. google/gemini-2.5-flash-lite or its current successor; reasoning OFF; temperature 0): ONE call per item with up to 20 numbered situations; the prompt shows ONLY the sentence and the situations (never a formula) and asks, for each situation, TRUE / FALSE / UNDETERMINED ('the situation does not say enough to decide'), returning strict JSON {\\\"1\\\":\\\"TRUE\\\",...}; parse, retry once on invalid JSON, else mark that item's LLM judge a coverage failure\\n   ROBUSTNESS LLM: a second family (e.g. qwen/qwen3-30b-a3b-instruct or meta-llama/llama-3.3-70b-instruct) on a 300-item sha1 subsample of track L\\n   BATCHING CHECK: 100 items re-judged one world per call; report the agreement with batched verdicts\\nscores per item (for each judge j ∈ {nli_M1, nli_M2, llm, llm2}):\\n   n_worlds; determined = worlds judged TRUE/FALSE\\n   b2_mismatch_j = (# determined worlds with judge truth != φ truth) / max(1, #determined)\\n   b2_undet_j = (# UNDETERMINED) / n_worlds   # pre-registered DROP/coverage signal\\n   b2_score_j = b2_mismatch_j * (1 - b2_undet_j) + 0.5 * b2_undet_j   # pre-registered combination: undetermined counts half\\n   also per-direction mismatch (W+ vs W-) and per-operator mismatch, used as the error-type readout: the operator whose worlds disagree most = predicted type\\ncoverage_status: 'ok' | 'unparseable' | 'no_mutants' | 'no_world' (every world build failed or timed out) | 'too_big' | 'judge_fail'; for AUROC, every non-ok item gets the MAX error score (flagged), and coverage is also reported separately\\nGATE (world-judging accuracy): run the same B2 pipeline with the REFERENCE formula ρ in place of φ, on track-H corrected gold (302) + the distinct track-L references; truth of the sentence in each world := ρ's truth (exact); accuracy_j = fraction of worlds where the judge answers exactly that truth (UNDETERMINED counts as wrong; its rate is reported separately); table by words stratum × judge × operator × direction; target >= 0.85; if NLI accuracy < 0.70 on >=20-word sentences, record it and declare b2_score_llm the PRIMARY B2 score (this rule is written in prereg.json)\\n\\n# ===== 5. SECONDARY TRACKS =====\\nSYNTH (for the C3 MIX term; never pooled with real-error AUROC): for each track-H corrected gold formula and each census operator applicable (NEG, REV, QUANT, RESTR, CONN, DROP, SWAP, SCOPE, ADD-of-a-foreign-atom drawn from another item of the same story), one sha1-chosen perturbation that is z3-non-equivalent to the reference; score B1 and B2(NLI + LLM); per-operator synthetic sensitivity at the pre-registered threshold\\nDISGUISE (contamination): disguise(text, fol) = sha1-seeded consistent map from each content lemma (spaCy lemma, POS NOUN/VERB/ADJ/PROPN) that matches a predicate/constant token (by lemma or WordNet derivation) to a pronounceable nonce word (CVCV+CVC from a fixed syllable table); replace all inflections in the text and the matching token inside the predicate/constant names; function words, quantifier words, 'not/if/unless' untouched; assert that the mapping is consistent on all items (a text word maps to one nonce everywhere); run B1 (M1, M3) and B2 (NLI M1 + LLM) on disguised track-L + track-H; contamination delta = AUROC(orig) - AUROC(disguised), paired bootstrap; expect M3 (FOLIO-trained) to show the largest delta\\nINVARIANCE: score B1/B2 on the 150×5 rewrites; FA_family = fraction flagged at the pre-registered threshold; also flip_rate = flagged(rewrite) & not flagged(original)\\nLOCAL BASELINES on identical items (reuse iter-3 code): L1 lint (any smell → 1), bag-of-words content accounting (content_accounting.py score), parse_ok, and formula length (#atoms) as a trivial control\\n\\n# ===== 6. ANALYSIS (analysis.py) =====\\nprimary set = track-L items with label ∈ {CORRECT, ERROR} (UNCERTAIN, UNPARSEABLE and TIMEOUT reported separately; UNPARSEABLE also as coverage)\\nfor each score: AUROC, AUPRC (+ prevalence), precision/recall/FA at the prereg threshold, precision re-weighted to 10% and 25% prevalence; paired bootstrap 2,000 resamples clustered by norm(text), percentile 95% CI; ΔAUROC vs each local baseline and B2 vs B1, paired\\ntie-aware pairwise accuracy (Deutsch et al. 2023 acc_eq, with tie calibration epsilon chosen on track H) for the quantised B2 scores and the binary L1\\ncross-fitted fusion: logistic regression on [B1 features + B2 features], GroupKFold(5) by sentence, fitted on track H and applied to track L (primary), plus within-track-L cross-fit (secondary); ΔAUROC of [L1 + bag-of-words + B] over [L1 + bag-of-words] (complementarity to FOL-Triage's cheap layers)\\nper-census-type sensitivity on track H (repair_census classes: ADD, DROP, CONN, UNGLUE, NEG, MOVE, SWAP, COMPOUND, ...) at the prereg threshold, with Wilson CIs; the same on track-L ERROR items by repair op; b1_t2f vs b1_f2t sensitivity split by DROP vs ADD (direction hypothesis)\\nerror-type readout: for single-op track-L/H errors, agreement between B2's argmax-disagreeing operator and the census operator (confusion matrix)\\npaired AUROC on the 99 track-H pairs (original vs corrected, same text) for comparison with iter-3's 0.59 bag-of-words\\ncomplexity: AUROC/FA by words stratum, n_quant, depth, n_conditions, exception; logistic slope of the score's discrimination vs log words (descriptive); a stratum is TESTED only if >=50 ERROR and >=50 CORRECT\\nper-system (gpt-3.5, gpt-4, davinci) AUROC and FA (descriptive if <50/50)\\nC3 inputs: per-operator synthetic sensitivity (SYNTH) vs real sensitivity (track H by census class), census-reweighted synthetic sensitivity vs observed real sensitivity\\ncost: OpenRouter $ per item (ledger), NLI GPU-seconds per item × A4500 $/h (log the rate used), z3 CPU-seconds per item; cost gate $0.002/item\\n\\n# ===== 7. OUTPUTS =====\\nmethod_out.json: {meta: {prereg_sha, match_rate, counts by track/label/system, budget_spent}, items: [{item_id, track, system, label, label_fine, strata, b1_min, b1_t2f, b1_f2t, b1_contra, b1_m2_*, b1_m3_*, b2_mismatch_nli, b2_undet_nli, b2_score_nli, b2_mismatch_llm, b2_undet_llm, b2_score_llm, b2_llm2_* (subset), b2_pred_op, n_worlds, n_mutants, coverage_status, cost_usd, seconds, disguised_{...}}], analysis: {auroc_table, fusion, per_type, invariance, gate_table, contamination, complexity, cost, synth_vs_real}} ; validate with aii-json (exp_gen_sol_out schema if applicable) and generate full/mini/preview; split with aii-file-size-limit if > limit\\nalso out/worlds_sample.jsonl (100 verbalised worlds with judge verdicts, for human inspection), data/screen_items.json, prereg.json, README.md (layout, how to run, reusable API docstrings), .aii/manifest.yaml (hf_cache/ and .venv/ -> delete redownloadable; out/ and data/ -> keep)\\nPUBLIC API (src/b_metrics.py, each with a precise docstring of what it measures): verbalize(fol)->str; b1_score(text, fol)->{t2f,f2t,min,contra}; build_worlds(fol, k=10)->list[{facts, phi_truth, op, direction}]; verbalize_world(facts)->str; b2_score(text, fol, judge='nli'|'llm')->{mismatch, undet, score, pred_op, n_worlds, coverage_status}; disguise(text, fol)->(text', fol')\",\n  \"fallback_plan\": \"F1 LOGIC-LM FILES: if the raw GitHub URL fails, clone the repo shallowly or use the GitHub API contents endpoint. If the record count is far below 204 (one web fetch reported 63; verify), use what exists, log it, and rely on the premise extension of the shared screen; do NOT pull other systems' outputs into track L (that would break byte-identity with the siblings). F2 LOW MATCH RATE (<150 distinct conclusions): apply the strategy's premise extension exactly as written. If track L still has fewer than 50 ERROR items, report the track-L AUROC as underpowered, and make track H plus the pooled L+H the descriptive headline while keeping track L as the pre-registered primary. F3 NLI FAILS THE WORLD GATE (<0.70 on >=20-word sentences or overall): per prereg, switch the primary B2 score to the LLM judge. If the LLM also scores <0.85 overall, report B2 as a negative (text-only judges cannot read templated worlds reliably), with the gate table broken down by operator/direction/stratum. Try ONE pre-declared alternative world format on 100 gate items: a compact table-like listing ('Students: Bonnie. Not students: thing1.'). Adopt it only if it improves gate accuracy by >=5 points, and log the switch as a post-hoc deviation. F4 B1 COLLAPSES ON TERSE PREDICATES (f2t near 0 for CORRECT items because 'Bonnie performs' does not entail 'Bonnie performs in school talent shows often'): this is the predicted failure. Report the B1-gloss diagnostic to quantify it, keep t2f (the DROP detector) as the deployable B1 score if f2t is uninformative, and state that B1 is vocabulary-sensitive. F5 z3 WORLD BUILDING SLOW OR FAILING: reduce K to 6 mutants, cap the domain at 3 plus constants, lower the Optimize timeout to 1 s, and fall back to Solver() without minimisation. Run a ProcessPoolExecutor with spawn and 6 workers. If coverage drops below 90%, report it: the coverage gate is part of the result, not something to engineer around. F6 GPU UNAVAILABLE: run M2 (base) for everything on CPU, and M1 only for B1 and the gate subset, logging the substitution. F7 BUDGET: the pre-sweep estimate must be <= $4 (expected ≈ $1.5: ~3.5k batched calls × ~1.8k input tokens at ~$0.10/M plus output). Check the ledger after every call. At $6 stop the robustness LLM and the batching check; at $9 stop all LLM calls and report NLI-only B2 for the remaining items with coverage_status 'judge_fail'. F8 LABELLER TIMEOUTS: items whose label search exceeds 25 s become UNCERTAIN(COMPOUND) exactly as in repair_census.search; equivalence timeouts become TIMEOUT_UNKNOWN; neither is merged with CORRECT. F9 DISGUISE BREAKS SENTENCES: if more than 20% of items have no text-predicate lemma match (nothing to disguise), report contamination only on the items with at least 2 mapped lemmas, with that n.\",\n  \"testing_plan\": \"T0 UNIT TESTS (must pass before any sweep; pytest in tests/): (a) parser precedence: 'A(x) ⊕ B(x) → C(x)' parses as imp(xor(A,B),C), '∀x (A(x)) ∧ ∀y (B(y))' parses as two conjoined sentences, and the 302 curated references parse at >= the iter-3 rate (8 known failures); (b) labeller regression: re-running the census labeller on the 99 track-H pairs reproduces iter-3 repair_census.rows.json classes except the precedence-affected items (expect about 3 COMPOUND->repaired; list every diff); (c) verbalize on 20 hand-written formulas including ∃x(A(x)→B(x)), ∀x(A(x)∧B(x)), ∀x(A(x)∧B(x)↔C(x)), xor, nested quantifiers, 0-ary and 3-ary atoms: assert the literal non-repaired forms and that no two non-identical ASTs give the same string (injectivity check over the 302 references); (d) worlds: for 50 random (φ,ψ) pairs, the Python finite-model evaluator agrees with the intended φ truth in every returned world, and φ≠ψ truth holds in each; (e) NLI sanity: 3 known pairs per model ('A man sleeps.'/'A person sleeps.' entail; '... is awake' contradiction) come out with the right labels via config.id2label; (f) disguise: the round trip keeps FOL parseable and z3-equivalent under renaming; the text keeps its logic words. T1 MINI RUN (30 track-L + 20 track-H items, sha1-first): end-to-end B1+B2 with NLI and LLM. Inspect 20 verbalisations and 20 verbalised worlds by printing them to logs; measure seconds/item and $/item, then extrapolate to the full run (must fit <= 2.5 h wall-clock and <= $4) before scaling. T2 GATE PILOT (100 reference worlds, stratified by words): compute judge accuracy for NLI-M1 and the LLM. It is a go signal if the LLM is >= 0.75 on the pilot. If both are < 0.6, stop scaling B2 and move to F3 early, so the time budget goes to B1 and the analysis. T3 SCALE 200 -> FULL per aii-long-running-tasks, running in the background with PID tracking and time logged per stage. The same guard applies to prereg thresholds: calibrate on track-H CORRECT items whose sentence is not in track L (fall back to all track-H CORRECT only if n < 60, and log that)", "originalFile": null, "structuredPatch": [{"oldStart": 5, "oldLines": 7, "newStart": 5, "newLines": 7, "lines": ["   \"domain_practice\": \"WHAT I READ: the neurosymbolic handbook (critical rules: never score on FOLIO/MALLS as shipped, ~39%/36% gold wrong per 2606.02837; compilation/parse is not faithfulness per 2604.19459/2606.16541; gold-free roundtrip certification is OCCUPIED by 2604.25031); the strategy's domain reasoning (TRUE, Honovich et al. NAACL 2022; FRANK, Pagnoni et al. 2021; Goyal & Durrett 2021; Deutsch, Foster & Freitag EMNLP 2023 'Ties Matter'); the Logic-LLM repo (outputs/logic_programs/FOLIO_dev_{gpt-3.5-turbo,gpt-4,text-davinci-003}.json; records have keys id, context, question, answer, options, raw_logic_programs, where each program is a text block with 'Predicates:', 'Premises:' and 'Conclusion:' sections of 'FOL ::: NL' lines, and the Predicates section carries a system-written English gloss per predicate); the MoritzLaurer/DeBERTa-v3-large-mnli-fever-anli-ling-wanli card (labels entailment/neutral/contradiction, MNLI 91.2/90.8, ANLI-all 70.2, ANLI-R3 64.0); the tasksource NLI cards (deberta-base-long-nli / deberta-v3-large-tasksource-nli are TRAINED ON FOLIO, FOL-nli and LogicNLI, i.e. contaminated for this screen); Raza & Milic-Frayling IJCAI 2025 (arXiv 2501.16961, Semantic Self-Verification: the LLM generates concrete instantiations of the problem, the solver checks the formalisation against them, with near-perfect precision over part of the cases). HOW THIS KIND OF STUDY IS BUILT. (1) BASELINES every reference-free faithfulness paper reports: an LLM judge (cheap and strong), NLI-based entailment scores (SummaC/TRUE-style: NLI between source and output, taken in both directions for coverage), a QA/probe-based metric, round-trip similarity, and trivial baselines (length, parse/compile rate). For formal outputs the field adds solver checks such as round-trip equivalence (2604.25031) and sampling consensus (2410.20936). The first comparison a reviewer names for an NLI-on-verbalisation metric is round-trip with an LLM verbaliser (sibling D computes it) and the plain LLM judge. The fair-tuning convention is one fixed prompt per model with no tuning on the evaluation labels, and thresholds fixed on a separate calibration set. (2) DATA: FOLIO and MALLS are the standard sentence-level NL->FOL corpora, usable only with the 2606.02837 corrected gold. Logic-LM's released outputs are the standard real-system outputs on FOLIO. ProverQA is template-generated and has no long conditioned sentences (iter-3 probe: 0/8,948). (3) CONTROLS: the same items and labels for every metric (paired design); the NLI checkpoint and prompt fixed before labels are seen; contamination checked with disguised or renamed items; invariance checked with meaning-preserving rewrites. (4) HOW MUCH IS ENOUGH: TRUE/SummaC-style meta-evaluations report ROC AUC on a few hundred to a few thousand binary-labelled items, with bootstrap CIs. Below about 50 positives and 50 negatives per cell nobody reads a per-stratum AUROC as more than descriptive. With about 150 errors and 350 correct items, the AUROC SE is about 0.025 and the minimum detectable paired ΔAUROC is about 0.06-0.07. (5) MEASURES: item-level ROC AUC (threshold-free) is the primary measure, with AUPRC always stated alongside its prevalence. Precision/recall/false-alarm rate are reported at a threshold fixed in advance. Binary or quantised scores are compared with tie-aware pairwise accuracy (Deutsch et al. 2023). Uncertainty comes from a paired bootstrap resampled by sentence cluster. Per-error-type sensitivity is reported in the FRANK tradition, and cost in $ and seconds per item.\",", "   \"practice_alignment\": \"MEETS: (a) real system outputs as the primary evidence (Logic-LM, 3 LLMs) with corrected or double-annotated references, never original FOLIO gold alone; (b) item-level AUROC primary, AUPRC with prevalence, sentence-clustered paired bootstrap (2,000 resamples), tie-aware pairwise accuracy for the quantised B2 scores; (c) thresholds fixed on a calibration set disjoint from the selection measure (track-H corrected gold), plus prereg.json written before track-L labels are read; (d) bidirectional NLI in the SummaC/TRUE tradition, with the NLI checkpoint named and a second, uncontaminated checkpoint as a robustness check; (e) contamination control via nonce-disguised items, and an explicit flag that tasksource NLI models were trained on FOLIO; (f) invariance under 5 STRICT rewrite families; (g) coverage failures kept in the denominator and scored as flagged; (h) per-type sensitivity on the census classes (FRANK-style); (i) cost in $ and GPU/CPU seconds per item. DEPARTURES. (1) No LLM-judge or round-trip baseline is computed IN this artifact. Sibling experiment D computes them on identical item_ids, and iteration 2's evaluation joins them for the ΔAUROC-over-judge rule. Cost: this artifact alone cannot claim incremental value over the judge. It reports only incremental value over the local cheap baselines it can run (bag-of-words content accounting and the L1 lint, both reused from iter-3 code) and over each other (B2 over B1). (2) Labels are automatic (modulo-vocabulary equivalence plus depth-2 typed repair), not panel-adjudicated, per the strategy's wide-screen trade. The dataset artifact adjudicates in parallel. Cost: aligner over-acceptance (e.g. Sugar->MadeFromSugar) can place real errors in CORRECT, and B1/B2, being text-aware, are the metrics most likely to flag exactly those, which would make them look falsely noisy. Mitigation: label_fine is emitted so that iteration 2 can re-score, and the GRAN-bridge cases are held out as UNCERTAIN in the primary analysis. (3) The long, heavily conditioned stratum is untestable here (FOLIO has about 19 such premises); it is reported descriptively and the claim is deferred to the held-out set. (4) Screen depth is one template set, one prompt and no hyperparameter search. A narrow loss (<0.03 AUROC) is carried forward, not killed. (5) Power: track L will probably hold 100-200 errors, which makes the per-stratum and per-system cells underpowered. The plan therefore pre-declares the testable cells (>=50/50) and labels the rest descriptive, rather than adding more metric variants. (6) B2 overlaps with SSV (Raza & Milic-Frayling 2025) in the solver+instantiation idea, but the direction is reversed: the solver makes the worlds and a text-only judge evaluates them, where SSV has the LLM make instances and the solver check them. It is also used as a gold-free SCORE rather than as a generation-time filter. This is cited as closest prior art, not claimed as a first.\",", "   \"builds_on\": \"REUSES FROM THE RUN (all read-only; copy into the workspace, never edit in place). (1) DATA already on disk in /ai-inventor/aii_data/runs/run_u75jRHUss0zo/iter_3/gen_hypo/claude_agent/data/: DSAVlab-UNIUD_FOLIO_validation-curated__FOLIO_instances.jsonl (keys id, NL_sentence, FOL_sentence = corrected, FOL_sentence_old = original, corrected, ambiguity, ambiguity_explanation, label, label_old; 73 rows whose id starts with 'story' are story/premise rows, the rest are conclusions); DSAVlab-UNIUD_MALLS_test_subset-CURATED__MALLS_instances.jsonl (NL_sentence, FOL_sentence_old, FOL_sentence_new, ambiguity); folio_refined_validation.csv (columns: nl premises, fol premises, nl conclusion, fol conclusion, label; newline-separated premise lists); yuan-yang__MALLS-v0__MALLS-v0.1-test.json. (2) CODE from .../iter_3/gen_hypo/claude_agent/probes/: fol.py (tokenize/parse to tuple AST ('all'|'ex', var, body), ('not', e), (op, a, b), ('atom', name, args); to_z3; equivalent; profile, i.e. z3 monotonicity UP/DOWN/VACUOUS/NONMONO), repair_census.py (align, gran_bridge, reify_expand, fp fingerprint, subterms/replace, edits(e, target) = the typed census operators NEG/QUANT/SCOPE/UNGLUE/MOVE/REV/RESTR/CONN/DROP/SWAP/BIND/ADD, search = depth<=2 minimal repair, work = the labeller), lint_smells.py (L1 smells, used here only as a complementary local baseline) and content_accounting.py (bag-of-words L2, the local coverage baseline whose iter-3 paired AUROC was 0.59). Known bug to fix first: fol.py PREC gives xor the same precedence as iff; set PREC = {'iff':1,'imp':2,'xor':3,'or':3,'and':4} so that ⊕ binds like ∨, tighter than →. (3) FINDINGS BUILT ON: the census (coverage ADD+DROP = 57% of repaired real errors; polarity 10%; 46% COMPOUND) is why B targets coverage and COMPOUND; bag-of-words L2 failed the coverage gate (0.32 sensitivity at <=10% FA), which is the bar B1 must beat; iter-2 found Udep2Mono polarity conflicts with FOL restrictor conventions, so B deliberately uses no polarity marker. (4) The strategy's SHARED SCREEN definition (gen_strat_1/.terminal_claude_agent_struct_out.json, 'rationale') is implemented verbatim so iteration 2 can join by item_id. EXTERNAL: Logic-LM released outputs (GitHub teacherpeterpan/Logic-LLM, outputs/logic_programs/FOLIO_dev_*.json). If any iter-3 file is missing, re-download the DSAVlab-UNIUD HF datasets and yfxiao/folio-refined with aii-hf-datasets and re-implement the parser from the AST spec above. This is not a fresh line: it is one of the four WIDEN candidates of invention-loop iteration 1, built on the iter-3 infrastructure.\",", "-  \"implementation_pseudocode\": \"# ===== 0. SETUP (gpu_basic; Python 3.12; uv) =====\\n# uv pip install z3-solver torch transformers accelerate sentencepiece protobuf nltk spacy lemminflect scikit-learn numpy pandas aiohttp loguru tqdm\\n# python -m spacy download en_core_web_sm ; nltk.download('wordnet'); nltk.download('omw-1.4')\\n# Read skills aii-python, aii-parallel-computing, aii-use-hardware, aii-long-running-tasks, aii-openrouter-llms, aii-json before coding.\\n# Layout: src/{fol.py, repair_census.py, lint_smells.py, content_accounting.py (copied), screen.py, verbalize.py, worlds.py, nli.py, llm_judge.py, disguise.py, rewrites.py, analysis.py, b_metrics.py (public API)}, data/, out/, logs/, prereg.json, method_out.json\\n# Global: seed 0; every sampling ordered by sha1; cost ledger out/cost_ledger.jsonl appended after EVERY OpenRouter call; HARD_STOP_USD = 9.0.\\n\\n# ===== 1. SHARED SCREEN (identical to siblings A/C/D/E) =====\\ncopy iter_3 fol.py, repair_census.py; patch PREC xor->3; unit-test parse('A(x) ⊕ B(x) → C(x)') == ('imp', ('xor',A,B), C)\\ndef norm(t): lowercase, collapse whitespace, strip punctuation incl. trailing '.', unify quotes\\ndownload Logic-LM FOLIO_dev_{gpt-3.5-turbo,gpt-4,text-davinci-003}.json (raw.githubusercontent.com/teacherpeterpan/Logic-LLM/main/outputs/logic_programs/...)\\nfor each record r, prog in r['raw_logic_programs'][0]: split sections by 'Premises:' / 'Conclusion:'; each line 'FOL ::: NL' -> (nl, fol) with role premise|conclusion  # IGNORE the 'Predicates:' glosses for the main metrics (not available in the user's operating situation); keep them in a side file for the B1-gloss diagnostic only\\ncurated_concl = {norm(NL_sentence): row for DSAVlab FOLIO rows whose id does not start with 'story'}  # reference = FOL_sentence (corrected)\\nagreed_premises = for each story row: split NL_sentence / FOL_sentence_old lines; find the same story in folio_refined_validation.csv (match on the norm of the first premise); pair lines by norm(nl); keep a premise if parse OK on both and equivalent_modulo_vocab(orig, refined) is True -> reference = the original-gold line\\nTRACK L: every Logic-LM (system, nl, fol) with norm(nl) in curated_concl (reference = corrected gold)\\n   log match_rate = distinct matched conclusions / total Logic-LM conclusions\\n   if distinct matched conclusions < 150: ALSO add Logic-LM premises of matched stories with norm(nl) in agreed_premises (the ONLY allowed extension); else premises are still added (the strategy also admits premise items; record ref_type)\\nTRACK H: 302 curated items (202 FOLIO conclusions + 100 MALLS): candidate = FOL_sentence_old, reference = corrected; ambiguity True -> label READING_CHOICE\\nitem_id = sha1(system + '|' + norm(text) + '|' + candidate_fol)[:16]  (system = 'gpt-4' etc. for L; 'human_FOLIO'/'human_MALLS' for H)\\ndef equivalent_modulo_vocab(c, r): ao,_,_ = align(c, r); if equivalent(ao, r) is True -> 'VOCAB'; gb = gran_bridge(ao, r); if gb and equivalent(*gb) is True -> 'GRAN'; else False (None on timeout)\\ndef label(cand_str, ref_str):\\n   try parse both except -> 'UNPARSEABLE' (a candidate parse failure; a reference parse failure -> drop the item and log it)\\n   eq = equivalent(c, r, 5000): True -> fine='EXACT', coarse='CORRECT'; None -> 'TIMEOUT_UNKNOWN'\\n   v = equivalent_modulo_vocab: 'VOCAB' -> CORRECT; 'GRAN' -> UNCERTAIN (fine GRAN)\\n   ops = search(base, tgt, budget_s=25): ops -> ERROR (fine 'ERR:'+'+'.join(ops)); None -> UNCERTAIN (fine COMPOUND)\\n   # emit BOTH label (coarse) and label_fine so that iteration 2 can re-map if siblings resolved 'VOCAB/GRAN-borderline' differently\\nstrata(text, ref_fol): words (<12 / 12-19 / >=20), n_quant, nesting depth (max AST depth of connectives), n_conditions (number of conjuncts in antecedents/restrictors of the reference), exception marker (regex unless|except|excluding|other than|but not|without)\\nINVARIANCE SET: 150 track-L CORRECT items, first by item_id sort; STRICT rewrites (seed 0): RENAME (each predicate -> WordNet synonym of its head token of equal arity, or an '_x' suffix if none; constants -> case/underscore variant), REORDER (swap operands of every ∧/∨), DEMORGAN (push one ¬ through ∧/∨ or introduce ¬¬-free De Morgan at the top connective), PRENEX (pull quantifiers out where no capture occurs), CONTRAPOS (A→B => ¬B→¬A at the top implication); z3-verify each non-rename rewrite as equivalent to the original; drop and count failures\\nwrite data/screen_items.json [{item_id, track, system, text, candidate_fol, reference_fol, ref_type, label, label_fine, repair_ops, ambiguous, strata, story_id}] + sha256 of the label vector in the log\\n\\n# ===== 2. PRE-REGISTRATION (before any score is joined to track-L labels) =====\\nprereg.json = {timestamp, sha256 of src/*.py, verbalizer templates, NLI model ids+revisions, LLM model id + exact prompt, mutant operator list + sampling rule, world limits, score definitions + orientation (higher = more likely error), operating thresholds = the 90th percentile of each score on track-H items labelled CORRECT (known-good human gold; NOT track L), selection-relevant analyses, stratum testability rule (>=50 ERROR and >=50 CORRECT), bootstrap settings}\\n\\n# ===== 3. B1: template verbaliser + bidirectional NLI =====\\ndef pred_phrase(name, args):  # deterministic, non-repairing\\n   toks = split camelCase/snake/digits -> lower\\n   head = toks[0]\\n   if head is a verb per WordNet (any verb synset) and not (noun-first and no verb morphology) and not ending in -ed/-ing: VERB -> '{a0} ' + lemminflect 3sg(head) + rest + (' ' + a1 ... joined with ' and ' for arity>2)\\n   elif head endswith ('ed','ing') or WordNet adj/participle: '{a0} is ' + ' '.join(toks) + (' ' + a1)\\n   else NOUN: unary '{a0} is a/an ' + phrase ; binary '{a0} is ' + phrase + ' of ' + a1 ; arity>=3 '{a0}, {a1} and {a2} stand in the ' + phrase + ' relation'\\n   args: variables stay as 'x','y'; constants -> split camel/underscore, Title Case\\n   arity 0: 'it is the case that ' + phrase\\ndef verbalize(e):\\n   all v B  -> 'for every thing ' + v + ', ' + verbalize(B)\\n   ex v B   -> 'there is some thing ' + v + ' such that ' + verbalize(B)\\n   not atom -> atom phrase with 'is not'/'does not' ; not complex -> 'it is not the case that (' + ... + ')'\\n   imp -> 'if ' + A + ', then ' + B ; iff -> A + ' if and only if ' + B ; xor -> 'either ' + A + ' or ' + B + ', but not both' ; or -> A + ' or ' + B ; and -> A + ' and ' + B\\n   nested binaries parenthesised with commas only where a child is itself binary (to keep scope visible); NO simplification, NO restrictor repair: ∃x(A→B) -> 'there is some thing x such that if x is an A, then x is a B'\\n   capitalise the first letter, add '.'\\nNLI models: M1 = MoritzLaurer/DeBERTa-v3-large-mnli-fever-anli-ling-wanli (primary); M2 = cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-base (robustness, MNLI+SNLI, NOT FOLIO-trained); M3 = tasksource/deberta-base-long-nli (FOLIO-TRAINED: contamination probe only). Read label indices from model.config.id2label (never hard-code); fp16; batch 32 (halve on OOM); max_length 512 (M3: 1280); log the truncation count\\nb1_t2f = P_entail(premise=text, hyp=verb) ; b1_f2t = P_entail(premise=verb, hyp=text) ; b1_min = min ; b1_contra = max(P_contra both ways)\\noriented error scores: 1-b1_min, 1-b1_t2f (predicted DROP detector), 1-b1_f2t (predicted ADD detector), b1_contra\\nB1-gloss DIAGNOSTIC (track L only, clearly marked not-deployable): the same, but predicates rendered with Logic-LM's own 'Predicates:' gloss; it measures how much of B1's failure is predicate-name terseness\\n\\n# ===== 4. B2: solver-built distinguishing worlds judged against the TEXT =====\\nMUT_OPS = {NEG, REV, QUANT, RESTR, CONN, DROP, SWAP, SCOPE, MOVE, UNGLUE, BIND}  # ADD needs vocabulary outside φ; its role is played by DROP-mutant worlds (they vary an added conjunct) and by the UNDETERMINED signal\\ndef mutants(φ): cands = [(op,ψ) for op,ψ in edits(φ, φ) if op in MUT_OPS]; dedupe by str; drop ψ with fp(ψ)==fp(φ) and equivalent(ψ,φ,1500) is True (also drop None/timeouts and count them)\\n   order by sha1(item_id|op|str(ψ)); round-robin over operator types; keep K<=10 (at least 6 when available)\\ndef world(φ, ψ, want_phi_true):\\n   consts = constants of φ∪ψ ; for k_extra in 0..3 (total <= max(4, len(consts)+1), hard cap 6):\\n      D = z3.EnumSort('D', consts_names + ['thing1'..'thing{k}'])   # constants = distinct named elements (unique-names assumption)\\n      translate φ, ψ with a copy of fol.to_z3 parameterised by sort D (atoms over constants map to their enum element)\\n      goal = And(φ, Not(ψ)) if want_phi_true else And(Not(φ), ψ)\\n      opt = z3.Optimize(); opt.set(timeout=2000); opt.add(goal); opt.minimize(Sum(If(atom,1,0) for every ground atom))  # the smallest, most readable world\\n      if sat: return facts = {ground atom: bool}   # fall back to Solver() when Optimize is unknown\\n   return None\\nfor each (op,ψ): W+ = world(φ,ψ,True) (φ TRUE there), W- = world(φ,ψ,False) (φ FALSE); dedupe worlds by fact set; verify each world by a pure-Python evaluator of φ (fol AST over the finite domain) and assert that truth == the expected value\\ndef verbalize_world(facts): 'There are exactly N individuals: Bonnie, thing1 and thing2.' + for each unary/0-ary predicate and each individual, one positive or negative fact sentence via pred_phrase ('Bonnie is a student.' / 'thing1 is not a student.'); for binary+: the positive facts, then 'No other <phrase> facts hold.'; cap 60 facts, and if exceeded mark the world 'too_big' and skip it\\nJUDGE-NLI: premise = situation, hypothesis = text -> argmax label: entailment=TRUE, contradiction=FALSE, neutral=UNDETERMINED; keep the probabilities\\nJUDGE-LLM (OpenRouter; primary cheap model chosen with aii-openrouter-llms at <= $0.15/M input, e.g. google/gemini-2.5-flash-lite or its current successor; reasoning OFF; temperature 0): ONE call per item with up to 20 numbered situations; the prompt shows ONLY the sentence and the situations (never a formula) and asks, for each situation, TRUE / FALSE / UNDETERMINED ('the situation does not say enough to decide'), returning strict JSON {\\\"1\\\":\\\"TRUE\\\",...}; parse, retry once on invalid JSON, else mark that item's LLM judge a coverage failure\\n   ROBUSTNESS LLM: a second family (e.g. qwen/qwen3-30b-a3b-instruct or meta-llama/llama-3.3-70b-instruct) on a 300-item sha1 subsample of track L\\n   BATCHING CHECK: 100 items re-judged one world per call; report the agreement with batched verdicts\\nscores per item (for each judge j ∈ {nli_M1, nli_M2, llm, llm2}):\\n   n_worlds; determined = worlds judged TRUE/FALSE\\n   b2_mismatch_j = (# determined worlds with judge truth != φ truth) / max(1, #determined)\\n   b2_undet_j = (# UNDETERMINED) / n_worlds   # pre-registered DROP/coverage signal\\n   b2_score_j = b2_mismatch_j * (1 - b2_undet_j) + 0.5 * b2_undet_j   # pre-registered combination: undetermined counts half\\n   also per-direction mismatch (W+ vs W-) and per-operator mismatch, used as the error-type readout: the operator whose worlds disagree most = predicted type\\ncoverage_status: 'ok' | 'unparseable' | 'no_mutants' | 'no_world' (every world build failed or timed out) | 'too_big' | 'judge_fail'; for AUROC, every non-ok item gets the MAX error score (flagged), and coverage is also reported separately\\nGATE (world-judging accuracy): run the same B2 pipeline with the REFERENCE formula ρ in place of φ, on track-H corrected gold (302) + the distinct track-L references; truth of the sentence in each world := ρ's truth (exact); accuracy_j = fraction of worlds where the judge answers exactly that truth (UNDETERMINED counts as wrong; its rate is reported separately); table by words stratum × judge × operator × direction; target >= 0.85; if NLI accuracy < 0.70 on >=20-word sentences, record it and declare b2_score_llm the PRIMARY B2 score (this rule is written in prereg.json)\\n\\n# ===== 5. SECONDARY TRACKS =====\\nSYNTH (for the C3 MIX term; never pooled with real-error AUROC): for each track-H corrected gold formula and each census operator applicable (NEG, REV, QUANT, RESTR, CONN, DROP, SWAP, SCOPE, ADD-of-a-foreign-atom drawn from another item of the same story), one sha1-chosen perturbation that is z3-non-equivalent to the reference; score B1 and B2(NLI + LLM); per-operator synthetic sensitivity at the pre-registered threshold\\nDISGUISE (contamination): disguise(text, fol) = sha1-seeded consistent map from each content lemma (spaCy lemma, POS NOUN/VERB/ADJ/PROPN) that matches a predicate/constant token (by lemma or WordNet derivation) to a pronounceable nonce word (CVCV+CVC from a fixed syllable table); replace all inflections in the text and the matching token inside the predicate/constant names; function words, quantifier words, 'not/if/unless' untouched; assert that the mapping is consistent on all items (a text word maps to one nonce everywhere); run B1 (M1, M3) and B2 (NLI M1 + LLM) on disguised track-L + track-H; contamination delta = AUROC(orig) - AUROC(disguised), paired bootstrap; expect M3 (FOLIO-trained) to show the largest delta\\nINVARIANCE: score B1/B2 on the 150×5 rewrites; FA_family = fraction flagged at the pre-registered threshold; also flip_rate = flagged(rewrite) & not flagged(original)\\nLOCAL BASELINES on identical items (reuse iter-3 code): L1 lint (any smell → 1), bag-of-words content accounting (content_accounting.py score), parse_ok, and formula length (#atoms) as a trivial control\\n\\n# ===== 6. ANALYSIS (analysis.py) =====\\nprimary set = track-L items with label ∈ {CORRECT, ERROR} (UNCERTAIN, UNPARSEABLE and TIMEOUT reported separately; UNPARSEABLE also as coverage)\\nfor each score: AUROC, AUPRC (+ prevalence), precision/recall/FA at the prereg threshold, precision re-weighted to 10% and 25% prevalence; paired bootstrap 2,000 resamples clustered by norm(text), percentile 95% CI; ΔAUROC vs each local baseline and B2 vs B1, paired\\ntie-aware pairwise accuracy (Deutsch et al. 2023 acc_eq, with tie calibration epsilon chosen on track H) for the quantised B2 scores and the binary L1\\ncross-fitted fusion: logistic regression on [B1 features + B2 features], GroupKFold(5) by sentence, fitted on track H and applied to track L (primary), plus within-track-L cross-fit (secondary); ΔAUROC of [L1 + bag-of-words + B] over [L1 + bag-of-words] (complementarity to FOL-Triage's cheap layers)\\nper-census-type sensitivity on track H (repair_census classes: ADD, DROP, CONN, UNGLUE, NEG, MOVE, SWAP, COMPOUND, ...) at the prereg threshold, with Wilson CIs; the same on track-L ERROR items by repair op; b1_t2f vs b1_f2t sensitivity split by DROP vs ADD (direction hypothesis)\\nerror-type readout: for single-op track-L/H errors, agreement between B2's argmax-disagreeing operator and the census operator (confusion matrix)\\npaired AUROC on the 99 track-H pairs (original vs corrected, same text) for comparison with iter-3's 0.59 bag-of-words\\ncomplexity: AUROC/FA by words stratum, n_quant, depth, n_conditions, exception; logistic slope of the score's discrimination vs log words (descriptive); a stratum is TESTED only if >=50 ERROR and >=50 CORRECT\\nper-system (gpt-3.5, gpt-4, davinci) AUROC and FA (descriptive if <50/50)\\nC3 inputs: per-operator synthetic sensitivity (SYNTH) vs real sensitivity (track H by census class), census-reweighted synthetic sensitivity vs observed real sensitivity\\ncost: OpenRouter $ per item (ledger), NLI GPU-seconds per item × A4500 $/h (log the rate used), z3 CPU-seconds per item; cost gate $0.002/item\\n\\n# ===== 7.
