SETUP (gpu_basic; Python 3.12; uv) =====\\n# uv pip install z3-solver torch transformers accelerate sentencepiece protobuf nltk spacy lemminflect scikit-learn numpy pandas aiohttp loguru tqdm\\n# python -m spacy download en_core_web_sm ; nltk.download('wordnet'); nltk.download('omw-1.4')\\n# Read skills aii-python, aii-parallel-computing, aii-use-hardware, aii-long-running-tasks, aii-openrouter-llms, aii-json before coding.\\n# Layout: src/{fol.py, repair_census.py, lint_smells.py, content_accounting.py (copied), screen.py, verbalize.py, worlds.py, nli.py, llm_judge.py, disguise.py, rewrites.py, analysis.py, b_metrics.py (public API)}, data/, out/, logs/, prereg.json, method_out.json\\n# Global: seed 0; every sampling ordered by sha1; cost ledger out/cost_ledger.jsonl appended after EVERY OpenRouter call; HARD_STOP_USD = 9.0.\\n\\n# ===== 1. SHARED SCREEN (identical to siblings A/C/D/E) =====\\ncopy iter_3 fol.py, repair_census.py; patch PREC xor->3; unit-test parse('A(x) ⊕ B(x) → C(x)') == ('imp', ('xor',A,B), C)\\ndef norm(t): lowercase, collapse whitespace, strip punctuation incl. trailing '.', unify quotes\\ndownload Logic-LM FOLIO_dev_{gpt-3.5-turbo,gpt-4,text-davinci-003}.json (raw.githubusercontent.com/teacherpeterpan/Logic-LLM/main/outputs/logic_programs/...)\\nfor each record r, prog in r['raw_logic_programs'][0]: split sections by 'Premises:' / 'Conclusion:'; each line 'FOL ::: NL' -> (nl, fol) with role premise|conclusion  # IGNORE the 'Predicates:' glosses for the main metrics (not available in the user's operating situation); keep them in a side file for the B1-gloss diagnostic only\\ncurated_concl = {norm(NL_sentence): row for DSAVlab FOLIO rows whose id does not start with 'story'}  # reference = FOL_sentence (corrected)\\nagreed_premises = for each story row: split NL_sentence / FOL_sentence_old lines; find the same story in folio_refined_validation.csv (match on the norm of the first premise); pair lines by norm(nl); keep a premise if parse OK on both and equivalent_modulo_vocab(orig, refined) is True -> reference = the original-gold line\\nTRACK L: every Logic-LM (system, nl, fol) with norm(nl) in curated_concl (reference = corrected gold)\\n   log match_rate = distinct matched conclusions / total Logic-LM conclusions\\n   if distinct matched conclusions < 150: ALSO add Logic-LM premises of matched stories with norm(nl) in agreed_premises (the ONLY allowed extension); else premises are still added (the strategy also admits premise items; record ref_type)\\nTRACK H: 302 curated items (202 FOLIO conclusions + 100 MALLS): candidate = FOL_sentence_old, reference = corrected; ambiguity True -> label READING_CHOICE\\nitem_id = sha1(system + '|' + norm(text) + '|' + candidate_fol)[:16]  (system = 'gpt-4' etc. for L; 'human_FOLIO'/'human_MALLS' for H)\\ndef equivalent_modulo_vocab(c, r): ao,_,_ = align(c, r); if equivalent(ao, r) is True -> 'VOCAB'; gb = gran_bridge(ao, r); if gb and equivalent(*gb) is True -> 'GRAN'; else False (None on timeout)\\ndef label(cand_str, ref_str):\\n   try parse both except -> 'UNPARSEABLE' (a candidate parse failure; a reference parse failure -> drop the item and log it)\\n   eq = equivalent(c, r, 5000): True -> fine='EXACT', coarse='CORRECT'; None -> 'TIMEOUT_UNKNOWN'\\n   v = equivalent_modulo_vocab: 'VOCAB' -> CORRECT; 'GRAN' -> UNCERTAIN (fine GRAN)\\n   ops = search(base, tgt, budget_s=25): ops -> ERROR (fine 'ERR:'+'+'.join(ops)); None -> UNCERTAIN (fine COMPOUND)\\n   # emit BOTH label (coarse) and label_fine so that iteration 2 can re-map if siblings resolved 'VOCAB/GRAN-borderline' differently\\nstrata(text, ref_fol): words (<12 / 12-19 / >=20), n_quant, nesting depth (max AST depth of connectives), n_conditions (number of conjuncts in antecedents/restrictors of the reference), exception marker (regex unless|except|excluding|other than|but not|without)\\nINVARIANCE SET: 150 track-L CORRECT items, first by item_id sort; STRICT rewrites (seed 0): RENAME (each predicate -> WordNet synonym of its head token of equal arity, or an '_x' suffix if none; constants -> case/underscore variant), REORDER (swap operands of every ∧/∨), DEMORGAN (push one ¬ through ∧/∨ or introduce ¬¬-free De Morgan at the top connective), PRENEX (pull quantifiers out where no capture occurs), CONTRAPOS (A→B => ¬B→¬A at the top implication); z3-verify each non-rename rewrite as equivalent to the original; drop and count failures\\nwrite data/screen_items.json [{item_id, track, system, text, candidate_fol, reference_fol, ref_type, label, label_fine, repair_ops, ambiguous, strata, story_id}] + sha256 of the label vector in the log\\n\\n# ===== 2. PRE-REGISTRATION (before any score is joined to track-L labels) =====\\nprereg.json = {timestamp, sha256 of src/*.py, verbalizer templates, NLI model ids+revisions, LLM model id + exact prompt, mutant operator list + sampling rule, world limits, score definitions + orientation (higher = more likely error), operating thresholds = the 90th percentile of each score on track-H items labelled CORRECT (known-good human gold; NOT track L), selection-relevant analyses, stratum testability rule (>=50 ERROR and >=50 CORRECT), bootstrap settings}\\n\\n# ===== 3. B1: template verbaliser + bidirectional NLI =====\\ndef pred_phrase(name, args):  # deterministic, non-repairing\\n   toks = split camelCase/snake/digits -> lower\\n   head = toks[0]\\n   if head is a verb per WordNet (any verb synset) and not (noun-first and no verb morphology) and not ending in -ed/-ing: VERB -> '{a0} ' + lemminflect 3sg(head) + rest + (' ' + a1 ... joined with ' and ' for arity>2)\\n   elif head endswith ('ed','ing') or WordNet adj/participle: '{a0} is ' + ' '.join(toks) + (' ' + a1)\\n   else NOUN: unary '{a0} is a/an ' + phrase ; binary '{a0} is ' + phrase + ' of ' + a1 ; arity>=3 '{a0}, {a1} and {a2} stand in the ' + phrase + ' relation'\\n   args: variables stay as 'x','y'; constants -> split camel/underscore, Title Case\\n   arity 0: 'it is the case that ' + phrase\\ndef verbalize(e):\\n   all v B  -> 'for every thing ' + v + ', ' + verbalize(B)\\n   ex v B   -> 'there is some thing ' + v + ' such that ' + verbalize(B)\\n   not atom -> atom phrase with 'is not'/'does not' ; not complex -> 'it is not the case that (' + ... + ')'\\n   imp -> 'if ' + A + ', then ' + B ; iff -> A + ' if and only if ' + B ; xor -> 'either ' + A + ' or ' + B + ', but not both' ; or -> A + ' or ' + B ; and -> A + ' and ' + B\\n   nested binaries parenthesised with commas only where a child is itself binary (to keep scope visible); NO simplification, NO restrictor repair: ∃x(A→B) -> 'there is some thing x such that if x is an A, then x is a B'\\n   capitalise the first letter, add '.'\\nNLI models: M1 = MoritzLaurer/DeBERTa-v3-large-mnli-fever-anli-ling-wanli (primary); M2 = cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-base (robustness, MNLI+SNLI, NOT FOLIO-trained); M3 = tasksource/deberta-base-long-nli (FOLIO-TRAINED: contamination probe only). Read label indices from model.config.id2label (never hard-code); fp16; batch 32 (halve on OOM); max_length 512 (M3: 1280); log the truncation count\\nb1_t2f = P_entail(premise=text, hyp=verb) ; b1_f2t = P_entail(premise=verb, hyp=text) ; b1_min = min ; b1_contra = max(P_contra both ways)\\noriented error scores: 1-b1_min, 1-b1_t2f (predicted DROP detector), 1-b1_f2t (predicted ADD detector), b1_contra\\nB1-gloss DIAGNOSTIC (track L only, clearly marked not-deployable): the same, but predicates rendered with Logic-LM's own 'Predicates:' gloss; it measures how much of B1's failure is predicate-name terseness\\n\\n# ===== 4. B2: solver-built distinguishing worlds judged against the TEXT =====\\nMUT_OPS = {NEG, REV, QUANT, RESTR, CONN, DROP, SWAP, SCOPE, MOVE, UNGLUE, BIND}  # ADD needs vocabulary outside φ; its role is played by DROP-mutant worlds (they vary an added conjunct) and by the UNDETERMINED signal\\ndef mutants(φ): cands = [(op,ψ) for op,ψ in edits(φ, φ) if op in MUT_OPS]; dedupe by str; drop ψ with fp(ψ)==fp(φ) and equivalent(ψ,φ,1500) is True (also drop None/timeouts and count them)\\n   order by sha1(item_id|op|str(ψ)); round-robin over operator types; keep K<=10 (at least 6 when available)\\ndef world(φ, ψ, want_phi_true):\\n   consts = constants of φ∪ψ ; for k_extra in 0..3 (total <= max(4, len(consts)+1), hard cap 6):\\n      D = z3.EnumSort('D', consts_names + ['thing1'..'thing{k}'])   # constants = distinct named elements (unique-names assumption)\\n      translate φ, ψ with a copy of fol.to_z3 parameterised by sort D (atoms over constants map to their enum element)\\n      goal = And(φ, Not(ψ)) if want_phi_true else And(Not(φ), ψ)\\n      opt = z3.Optimize(); opt.set(timeout=2000); opt.add(goal); opt.minimize(Sum(If(atom,1,0) for every ground atom))  # the smallest, most readable world\\n      if sat: return facts = {ground atom: bool}   # fall back to Solver() when Optimize is unknown\\n   return None\\nfor each (op,ψ): W+ = world(φ,ψ,True) (φ TRUE there), W- = world(φ,ψ,False) (φ FALSE); dedupe worlds by fact set; verify each world by a pure-Python evaluator of φ (fol AST over the finite domain) and assert that truth == the expected value\\ndef verbalize_world(facts): 'There are exactly N individuals: Bonnie, thing1 and thing2.' + for each unary/0-ary predicate and each individual, one positive or negative fact sentence via pred_phrase ('Bonnie is a student.' / 'thing1 is not a student.'); for binary+: the positive facts, then 'No other <phrase> facts hold.'; cap 60 facts, and if exceeded mark the world 'too_big' and skip it\\nJUDGE-NLI: premise = situation, hypothesis = text -> argmax label: entailment=TRUE, contradiction=FALSE, neutral=UNDETERMINED; keep the probabilities\\nJUDGE-LLM (OpenRouter; primary cheap model chosen with aii-openrouter-llms at <= $0.15/M input, e.g. google/gemini-2.5-flash-lite or its current successor; reasoning OFF; temperature 0): ONE call per item with up to 20 numbered situations; the prompt shows ONLY the sentence and the situations (never a formula) and asks, for each situation, TRUE / FALSE / UNDETERMINED ('the situation does not say enough to decide'), returning strict JSON {\\\"1\\\":\\\"TRUE\\\",...}; parse, retry once on invalid JSON, else mark that item's LLM judge a coverage failure\\n   ROBUSTNESS LLM: a second family (e.g. qwen/qwen3-30b-a3b-instruct or meta-llama/llama-3.3-70b-instruct) on a 300-item sha1 subsample of track L\\n   BATCHING CHECK: 100 items re-judged one world per call; report the agreement with batched verdicts\\nscores per item (for each judge j ∈ {nli_M1, nli_M2, llm, llm2}):\\n   n_worlds; determined = worlds judged TRUE/FALSE\\n   b2_mismatch_j = (# determined worlds with judge truth != φ truth) / max(1, #determined)\\n   b2_undet_j = (# UNDETERMINED) / n_worlds   # pre-registered DROP/coverage signal\\n   b2_score_j = b2_mismatch_j * (1 - b2_undet_j) + 0.5 * b2_undet_j   # pre-registered combination: undetermined counts half\\n   also per-direction mismatch (W+ vs W-) and per-operator mismatch, used as the error-type readout: the operator whose worlds disagree most = predicted type\\ncoverage_status: 'ok' | 'unparseable' | 'no_mutants' | 'no_world' (every world build failed or timed out) | 'too_big' | 'judge_fail'; for AUROC, every non-ok item gets the MAX error score (flagged), and coverage is also reported separately\\nGATE (world-judging accuracy): run the same B2 pipeline with the REFERENCE formula ρ in place of φ, on track-H corrected gold (302) + the distinct track-L references; truth of the sentence in each world := ρ's truth (exact); accuracy_j = fraction of worlds where the judge answers exactly that truth (UNDETERMINED counts as wrong; its rate is reported separately); table by words stratum × judge × operator × direction; target >= 0.85; if NLI accuracy < 0.70 on >=20-word sentences, record it and declare b2_score_llm the PRIMARY B2 score (this rule is written in prereg.json)\\n\\n# ===== 5. SECONDARY TRACKS =====\\nSYNTH (for the C3 MIX term; never pooled with real-error AUROC): for each track-H corrected gold formula and each census operator applicable (NEG, REV, QUANT, RESTR, CONN, DROP, SWAP, SCOPE, ADD-of-a-foreign-atom drawn from another item of the same story), one sha1-chosen perturbation that is z3-non-equivalent to the reference; score B1 and B2(NLI + LLM); per-operator synthetic sensitivity at the pre-registered threshold\\nDISGUISE (contamination): disguise(text, fol) = sha1-seeded consistent map from each content lemma (spaCy lemma, POS NOUN/VERB/ADJ/PROPN) that matches a predicate/constant token (by lemma or WordNet derivation) to a pronounceable nonce word (CVCV+CVC from a fixed syllable table); replace all inflections in the text and the matching token inside the predicate/constant names; function words, quantifier words, 'not/if/unless' untouched; assert that the mapping is consistent on all items (a text word maps to one nonce everywhere); run B1 (M1, M3) and B2 (NLI M1 + LLM) on disguised track-L + track-H; contamination delta = AUROC(orig) - AUROC(disguised), paired bootstrap; expect M3 (FOLIO-trained) to show the largest delta\\nINVARIANCE: score B1/B2 on the 150×5 rewrites; FA_family = fraction flagged at the pre-registered threshold; also flip_rate = flagged(rewrite) & not flagged(original)\\nLOCAL BASELINES on identical items (reuse iter-3 code): L1 lint (any smell → 1), bag-of-words content accounting (content_accounting.py score), parse_ok, and formula length (#atoms) as a trivial control\\n\\n# ===== 6. ANALYSIS (analysis.py) =====\\nprimary set = track-L items with label ∈ {CORRECT, ERROR} (UNCERTAIN, UNPARSEABLE and TIMEOUT reported separately; UNPARSEABLE also as coverage)\\nfor each score: AUROC, AUPRC (+ prevalence), precision/recall/FA at the prereg threshold, precision re-weighted to 10% and 25% prevalence; paired bootstrap 2,000 resamples clustered by norm(text), percentile 95% CI; ΔAUROC vs each local baseline and B2 vs B1, paired\\ntie-aware pairwise accuracy (Deutsch et al. 2023 acc_eq, with tie calibration epsilon chosen on track H) for the quantised B2 scores and the binary L1\\ncross-fitted fusion: logistic regression on [B1 features + B2 features]. SENTENCE-LEAK GUARD: the 202 FOLIO conclusions are the SAME sentences in track H and track L, so the primary model is fitted on track H with every sentence whose norm(text) occurs in track L REMOVED (in practice MALLS-test + non-overlapping items; log the n) and then applied to track L. If the record count is far below 204 (one web fetch reported 63; verify), use what exists, log it, and rely on the premise extension of the shared screen; do NOT pull other systems' outputs into track L (that would break byte-identity with the siblings). F2 LOW MATCH RATE (<150 distinct conclusions): apply the strategy's premise extension exactly as written. If track L still has fewer than 50 ERROR items, report the track-L AUROC as underpowered, and make track H plus the pooled L+H the descriptive headline while keeping track L as the pre-registered primary. F3 NLI FAILS THE WORLD GATE (<0.70 on >=20-word sentences or overall): per prereg, switch the primary B2 score to the LLM judge. If the LLM also scores <0.85 overall, report B2 as a negative (text-only judges cannot read templated worlds reliably), with the gate table broken down by operator/direction/stratum. Try ONE pre-declared alternative world format on 100 gate items: a compact table-like listing ('Students: Bonnie. Not students: thing1.'). Adopt it only if it improves gate accuracy by >=5 points, and log the switch as a post-hoc deviation. F4 B1 COLLAPSES ON TERSE PREDICATES (f2t near 0 for CORRECT items because 'Bonnie performs' does not entail 'Bonnie performs in school talent shows often'): this is the predicted failure. Report the B1-gloss diagnostic to quantify it, keep t2f (the DROP detector) as the deployable B1 score if f2t is uninformative, and state that B1 is vocabulary-sensitive. F5 z3 WORLD BUILDING SLOW OR FAILING: reduce K to 6 mutants, cap the domain at 3 plus constants, lower the Optimize timeout to 1 s, and fall back to Solver() without minimisation. Run a ProcessPoolExecutor with spawn and 6 workers. If coverage drops below 90%, report it: the coverage gate is part of the result, not something to engineer around. F6 GPU UNAVAILABLE: run M2 (base) for everything on CPU, and M1 only for B1 and the gate subset, logging the substitution. F7 BUDGET: the pre-sweep estimate must be <= $4 (expected ≈ $1.5: ~3.5k batched calls × ~1.8k input tokens at ~$0.10/M plus output). Check the ledger after every call. At $6 stop the robustness LLM and the batching check; at $9 stop all LLM calls and report NLI-only B2 for the remaining items with coverage_status 'judge_fail'. F8 LABELLER TIMEOUTS: items whose label search exceeds 25 s become UNCERTAIN(COMPOUND) exactly as in repair_census.search; equivalence timeouts become TIMEOUT_UNKNOWN; neither is merged with CORRECT. F9 DISGUISE BREAKS SENTENCES: if more than 20% of items have no text-predicate lemma match (nothing to disguise), report contamination only on the items with at least 2 mapped lemmas, with that n.\",", "   \"testing_plan\": \"T0 UNIT TESTS (must pass before any sweep; pytest in tests/): (a) parser precedence: 'A(x) ⊕ B(x) → C(x)' parses as imp(xor(A,B),C), '∀x (A(x)) ∧ ∀y (B(y))' parses as two conjoined sentences, and the 302 curated references parse at >= the iter-3 rate (8 known failures); (b) labeller regression: re-running the census labeller on the 99 track-H pairs reproduces iter-3 repair_census.rows.json classes except the precedence-affected items (expect about 3 COMPOUND->repaired; list every diff); (c) verbalize on 20 hand-written formulas including ∃x(A(x)→B(x)), ∀x(A(x)∧B(x)), ∀x(A(x)∧B(x)↔C(x)), xor, nested quantifiers, 0-ary and 3-ary atoms: assert the literal non-repaired forms and that no two non-identical ASTs give the same string (injectivity check over the 302 references); (d) worlds: for 50 random (φ,ψ) pairs, the Python finite-model evaluator agrees with the intended φ truth in every returned world, and φ≠ψ truth holds in each; (e) NLI sanity: 3 known pairs per model ('A man sleeps.'/'A person sleeps.' entail; '... is awake' contradiction) come out with the right labels via config.id2label; (f) disguise: the round trip keeps FOL parseable and z3-equivalent under renaming; the text keeps its logic words. T1 MINI RUN (30 track-L + 20 track-H items, sha1-first): end-to-end B1+B2 with NLI and LLM. Inspect 20 verbalisations and 20 verbalised worlds by printing them to logs; measure seconds/item and $/item, then extrapolate to the full run (must fit <= 2.5 h wall-clock and <= $4) before scaling. T2 GATE PILOT (100 reference worlds, stratified by words): compute judge accuracy for NLI-M1 and the LLM. It is a go signal if the LLM is >= 0.75 on the pilot. If both are < 0.6, stop scaling B2 and move to F3 early, so the time budget goes to B1 and the analysis. T3 SCALE 200 -> FULL per aii-long-running-tasks, running in the background with PID tracking and time logged per stage. HOW THIS KIND OF STUDY IS BUILT. (1) BASELINES every reference-free faithfulness paper reports: an LLM judge (cheap and strong), NLI-based entailment scores (SummaC/TRUE-style: NLI between source and output, taken in both directions for coverage), a QA/probe-based metric, round-trip similarity, and trivial baselines (length, parse/compile rate). For formal outputs the field adds solver checks such as round-trip equivalence (2604.25031) and sampling consensus (2410.20936). The first comparison a reviewer names for an NLI-on-verbalisation metric is round-trip with an LLM verbaliser (sibling D computes it) and the plain LLM judge. The fair-tuning convention is one fixed prompt per model with no tuning on the evaluation labels, and thresholds fixed on a separate calibration set. (2) DATA: FOLIO and MALLS are the standard sentence-level NL->FOL corpora, usable only with the 2606.02837 corrected gold. Logic-LM's released outputs are the standard real-system outputs on FOLIO. ProverQA is template-generated and has no long conditioned sentences (iter-3 probe: 0/8,948). (3) CONTROLS: the same items and labels for every metric (paired design); the NLI checkpoint and prompt fixed before labels are seen; contamination checked with disguised or renamed items; invariance checked with meaning-preserving rewrites. (4) HOW MUCH IS ENOUGH: TRUE/SummaC-style meta-evaluations report ROC AUC on a few hundred to a few thousand binary-labelled items, with bootstrap CIs. Below about 50 positives and 50 negatives per cell nobody reads a per-stratum AUROC as more than descriptive. With about 150 errors and 350 correct items, the AUROC SE is about 0.025 and the minimum detectable paired ΔAUROC is about 0.06-0.07. (5) MEASURES: item-level ROC AUC (threshold-free) is the primary measure, with AUPRC always stated alongside its prevalence. Precision/recall/false-alarm rate are reported at a threshold fixed in advance. Binary or quantised scores are compared with tie-aware pairwise accuracy (Deutsch et al. 2023). Uncertainty comes from a paired bootstrap resampled by sentence cluster. DEPARTURES. (1) No LLM-judge or round-trip baseline is computed IN this artifact. Sibling experiment D computes them on identical item_ids, and iteration 2's evaluation joins them for the ΔAUROC-over-judge rule. Cost: this artifact alone cannot claim incremental value over the judge. It reports only incremental value over the local cheap baselines it can run (bag-of-words content accounting and the L1 lint, both reused from iter-3 code) and over each other (B2 over B1). (2) Labels are automatic (modulo-vocabulary equivalence plus depth-2 typed repair), not panel-adjudicated, per the strategy's wide-screen trade. The dataset artifact adjudicates in parallel. Cost: aligner over-acceptance (e.g. Sugar->MadeFromSugar) can place real errors in CORRECT, and B1/B2, being text-aware, are the metrics most likely to flag exactly those, which would make them look falsely noisy. Mitigation: label_fine is emitted so that iteration 2 can re-score, and the GRAN-bridge cases are held out as UNCERTAIN in the primary analysis. (3) The long, heavily conditioned stratum is untestable here (FOLIO has about 19 such premises); it is reported descriptively and the claim is deferred to the held-out set. (4) Screen depth is one template set, one prompt and no hyperparameter search. A narrow loss (<0.03 AUROC) is carried forward, not killed. (5) Power: track L will probably hold 100-200 errors, which makes the per-stratum and per-system cells underpowered. The plan therefore pre-declares the testable cells (>=50/50) and labels the rest descriptive, rather than adding more metric variants. (6) B2 overlaps with SSV (Raza & Milic-Frayling 2025) in the solver+instantiation idea, but the direction is reversed: the solver makes the worlds and a text-only judge evaluates them, where SSV has the LLM make instances and the solver check them. It is also used as a gold-free SCORE rather than as a generation-time filter. SENTENCE-LEAK GUARD: the 202 FOLIO conclusions are the SAME sentences in track H and track L, so the primary model is fitted on track H with every sentence whose norm(text) occurs in track L REMOVED (in practice MALLS-test + non-overlapping items; log the n) and then applied to track L. The secondary model is within-track-L GroupKFold(5) by sentence. OUTPUTS =====\\nmethod_out.json: {meta: {prereg_sha, match_rate, counts by track/label/system, budget_spent}, items: [{item_id, track, system, label, label_fine, strata, b1_min, b1_t2f, b1_f2t, b1_contra, b1_m2_*, b1_m3_*, b2_mismatch_nli, b2_undet_nli, b2_score_nli, b2_mismatch_llm, b2_undet_llm, b2_score_llm, b2_llm2_* (subset), b2_pred_op, n_worlds, n_mutants, coverage_status, cost_usd, seconds, disguised_{...}}], analysis: {auroc_table, fusion, per_type, invariance, gate_table, contamination, complexity, cost, synth_vs_real}} ; validate with aii-json (exp_gen_sol_out schema if applicable) and generate full/mini/preview; split with aii-file-size-limit if > limit\\nalso out/worlds_sample.jsonl (100 verbalised worlds with judge verdicts, for human inspection), data/screen_items.json, prereg.json, README.md (layout, how to run, reusable API docstrings), .aii/manifest.yaml (hf_cache/ and .venv/ -> delete redownloadable; out/ and data/ -> keep)\\nPUBLIC API (src/b_metrics.py, each with a precise docstring of what it measures): verbalize(fol)->str; b1_score(text, fol)->{t2f,f2t,min,contra}; build_worlds(fol, k=10)->list[{facts, phi_truth, op, direction}]; verbalize_world(facts)->str; b2_score(text, fol, judge='nli'|'llm')->{mismatch, undet, score, pred_op, n_worlds, coverage_status}; disguise(text, fol)->(text', fol')\",", "+  \"implementation_pseudocode\": \"# ===== 0. SETUP (gpu_basic; Python 3.12; uv) =====\\n# uv pip install z3-solver torch transformers accelerate sentencepiece protobuf nltk spacy lemminflect scikit-learn numpy pandas aiohttp loguru tqdm\\n# python -m spacy download en_core_web_sm ; nltk.download('wordnet'); nltk.download('omw-1.4')\\n# Read skills aii-python, aii-parallel-computing, aii-use-hardware, aii-long-running-tasks, aii-openrouter-llms, aii-json before coding.\\n# Layout: src/{fol.py, repair_census.py, lint_smells.py, content_accounting.py (copied), screen.py, verbalize.py, worlds.py, nli.py, llm_judge.py, disguise.py, rewrites.py, analysis.py, b_metrics.py (public API)}, data/, out/, logs/, prereg.json, method_out.json\\n# Global: seed 0; every sampling ordered by sha1; cost ledger out/cost_ledger.jsonl appended after EVERY OpenRouter call; HARD_STOP_USD = 9.0.\\n\\n# ===== 1. SHARED SCREEN (identical to siblings A/C/D/E) =====\\ncopy iter_3 fol.py, repair_census.py; patch PREC xor->3; unit-test parse('A(x) ⊕ B(x) → C(x)') == ('imp', ('xor',A,B), C)\\ndef norm(t): lowercase, collapse whitespace, strip punctuation incl. trailing '.', unify quotes\\ndownload Logic-LM FOLIO_dev_{gpt-3.5-turbo,gpt-4,text-davinci-003}.json (raw.githubusercontent.com/teacherpeterpan/Logic-LLM/main/outputs/logic_programs/...)\\nfor each record r, prog in r['raw_logic_programs'][0]: split sections by 'Premises:' / 'Conclusion:'; each line 'FOL ::: NL' -> (nl, fol) with role premise|conclusion  # IGNORE the 'Predicates:' glosses for the main metrics (not available in the user's operating situation); keep them in a side file for the B1-gloss diagnostic only\\ncurated_concl = {norm(NL_sentence): row for DSAVlab FOLIO rows whose id does not start with 'story'}  # reference = FOL_sentence (corrected)\\nagreed_premises = for each story row: split NL_sentence / FOL_sentence_old lines; find the same story in folio_refined_validation.csv (match on the norm of the first premise); pair lines by norm(nl); keep a premise if parse OK on both and equivalent_modulo_vocab(orig, refined) is True -> reference = the original-gold line\\nTRACK L: every Logic-LM (system, nl, fol) with norm(nl) in curated_concl (reference = corrected gold)\\n   log match_rate = distinct matched conclusions / total Logic-LM conclusions\\n   if distinct matched conclusions < 150: ALSO add Logic-LM premises of matched stories with norm(nl) in agreed_premises (the ONLY allowed extension); else premises are still added (the strategy also admits premise items; record ref_type)\\nTRACK H: 302 curated items (202 FOLIO conclusions + 100 MALLS): candidate = FOL_sentence_old, reference = corrected; ambiguity True -> label READING_CHOICE\\nitem_id = sha1(system + '|' + norm(text) + '|' + candidate_fol)[:16]  (system = 'gpt-4' etc. for L; 'human_FOLIO'/'human_MALLS' for H)\\ndef equivalent_modulo_vocab(c, r): ao,_,_ = align(c, r); if equivalent(ao, r) is True -> 'VOCAB'; gb = gran_bridge(ao, r); if gb and equivalent(*gb) is True -> 'GRAN'; else False (None on timeout)\\ndef label(cand_str, ref_str):\\n   try parse both except -> 'UNPARSEABLE' (a candidate parse failure; a reference parse failure -> drop the item and log it)\\n   eq = equivalent(c, r, 5000): True -> fine='EXACT', coarse='CORRECT'; None -> 'TIMEOUT_UNKNOWN'\\n   v = equivalent_modulo_vocab: 'VOCAB' -> CORRECT; 'GRAN' -> UNCERTAIN (fine GRAN)\\n   ops = search(base, tgt, budget_s=25): ops -> ERROR (fine 'ERR:'+'+'.join(ops)); None -> UNCERTAIN (fine COMPOUND)\\n   # emit BOTH label (coarse) and label_fine so that iteration 2 can re-map if siblings resolved 'VOCAB/GRAN-borderline' differently\\nstrata(text, ref_fol): words (<12 / 12-19 / >=20), n_quant, nesting depth (max AST depth of connectives), n_conditions (number of conjuncts in antecedents/restrictors of the reference), exception marker (regex unless|except|excluding|other than|but not|without)\\nINVARIANCE SET: 150 track-L CORRECT items, first by item_id sort; STRICT rewrites (seed 0): RENAME (each predicate -> WordNet synonym of its head token of equal arity, or an '_x' suffix if none; constants -> case/underscore variant), REORDER (swap operands of every ∧/∨), DEMORGAN (push one ¬ through ∧/∨ or introduce ¬¬-free De Morgan at the top connective), PRENEX (pull quantifiers out where no capture occurs), CONTRAPOS (A→B => ¬B→¬A at the top implication); z3-verify each non-rename rewrite as equivalent to the original; drop and count failures\\nwrite data/screen_items.json [{item_id, track, system, text, candidate_fol, reference_fol, ref_type, label, label_fine, repair_ops, ambiguous, strata, story_id}] + sha256 of the label vector in the log\\n\\n# ===== 2. PRE-REGISTRATION (before any score is joined to track-L labels) =====\\nprereg.json = {timestamp, sha256 of src/*.py, verbalizer templates, NLI model ids+revisions, LLM model id + exact prompt, mutant operator list + sampling rule, world limits, score definitions + orientation (higher = more likely error), operating thresholds = the 90th percentile of each score on track-H items labelled CORRECT whose sentence does not occur in track L (known-good human gold; NOT track L; see the leak guard in step 6), selection-relevant analyses, stratum testability rule (>=50 ERROR and >=50 CORRECT), bootstrap settings}\\n\\n# ===== 3. B1: template verbaliser + bidirectional NLI =====\\ndef pred_phrase(name, args):  # deterministic, non-repairing\\n   toks = split camelCase/snake/digits -> lower\\n   head = toks[0]\\n   if head is a verb per WordNet (any verb synset) and not (noun-first and no verb morphology) and not ending in -ed/-ing: VERB -> '{a0} ' + lemminflect 3sg(head) + rest + (' ' + a1 ... joined with ' and ' for arity>2)\\n   elif head endswith ('ed','ing') or WordNet adj/participle: '{a0} is ' + ' '.join(toks) + (' ' + a1)\\n   else NOUN: unary '{a0} is a/an ' + phrase ; binary '{a0} is ' + phrase + ' of ' + a1 ; arity>=3 '{a0}, {a1} and {a2} stand in the ' + phrase + ' relation'\\n   args: variables stay as 'x','y'; constants -> split camel/underscore, Title Case\\n   arity 0: 'it is the case that ' + phrase\\ndef verbalize(e):\\n   all v B  -> 'for every thing ' + v + ', ' + verbalize(B)\\n   ex v B   -> 'there is some thing ' + v + ' such that ' + verbalize(B)\\n   not atom -> atom phrase with 'is not'/'does not' ; not complex -> 'it is not the case that (' + ... + ')'\\n   imp -> 'if ' + A + ', then ' + B ; iff -> A + ' if and only if ' + B ; xor -> 'either ' + A + ' or ' + B + ', but not both' ; or -> A + ' or ' + B ; and -> A + ' and ' + B\\n   nested binaries parenthesised with commas only where a child is itself binary (to keep scope visible); NO simplification, NO restrictor repair: ∃x(A→B) -> 'there is some thing x such that if x is an A, then x is a B'\\n   capitalise the first letter, add '.'\\nNLI models: M1 = MoritzLaurer/DeBERTa-v3-large-mnli-fever-anli-ling-wanli (primary); M2 = cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-base (robustness, MNLI+SNLI, NOT FOLIO-trained); M3 = tasksource/deberta-base-long-nli (FOLIO-TRAINED: contamination probe only). Read label indices from model.config.id2label (never hard-code); fp16; batch 32 (halve on OOM); max_length 512 (M3: 1280); log the truncation count\\nb1_t2f = P_entail(premise=text, hyp=verb) ; b1_f2t = P_entail(premise=verb, hyp=text) ; b1_min = min ; b1_contra = max(P_contra both ways)\\noriented error scores: 1-b1_min, 1-b1_t2f (predicted DROP detector), 1-b1_f2t (predicted ADD detector), b1_contra\\nB1-gloss DIAGNOSTIC (track L only, clearly marked not-deployable): the same, but predicates rendered with Logic-LM's own 'Predicates:' gloss; it measures how much of B1's failure is predicate-name terseness\\n\\n# ===== 4. B2: solver-built distinguishing worlds judged against the TEXT =====\\nMUT_OPS = {NEG, REV, QUANT, RESTR, CONN, DROP, SWAP, SCOPE, MOVE, UNGLUE, BIND}  # ADD needs vocabulary outside φ; its role is played by DROP-mutant worlds (they vary an added conjunct) and by the UNDETERMINED signal\\ndef mutants(φ): cands = [(op,ψ) for op,ψ in edits(φ, φ) if op in MUT_OPS]; dedupe by str; drop ψ with fp(ψ)==fp(φ) and equivalent(ψ,φ,1500) is True (also drop None/timeouts and count them)\\n   order by sha1(item_id|op|str(ψ)); round-robin over operator types; keep K<=10 (at least 6 when available)\\ndef world(φ, ψ, want_phi_true):\\n   consts = constants of φ∪ψ ; for k_extra in 0..3 (total <= max(4, len(consts)+1), hard cap 6):\\n      D = z3.EnumSort('D', consts_names + ['thing1'..'thing{k}'])   # constants = distinct named elements (unique-names assumption)\\n      translate φ, ψ with a copy of fol.to_z3 parameterised by sort D (atoms over constants map to their enum element)\\n      goal = And(φ, Not(ψ)) if want_phi_true else And(Not(φ), ψ)\\n      opt = z3.Optimize(); opt.set(timeout=2000); opt.add(goal); opt.minimize(Sum(If(atom,1,0) for every ground atom))  # the smallest, most readable world\\n      if sat: return facts = {ground atom: bool}   # fall back to Solver() when Optimize is unknown\\n   return None\\nfor each (op,ψ): W+ = world(φ,ψ,True) (φ TRUE there), W- = world(φ,ψ,False) (φ FALSE); dedupe worlds by fact set; verify each world by a pure-Python evaluator of φ (fol AST over the finite domain) and assert that truth == the expected value\\ndef verbalize_world(facts): 'There are exactly N individuals: Bonnie, thing1 and thing2.' + for each unary/0-ary predicate and each individual, one positive or negative fact sentence via pred_phrase ('Bonnie is a student.' / 'thing1 is not a student.'); for binary+: the positive facts, then 'No other <phrase> facts hold.'; cap 60 facts, and if exceeded mark the world 'too_big' and skip it\\nJUDGE-NLI: premise = situation, hypothesis = text -> argmax label: entailment=TRUE, contradiction=FALSE, neutral=UNDETERMINED; keep the probabilities\\nJUDGE-LLM (OpenRouter; primary cheap model chosen with aii-openrouter-llms at <= $0.15/M input, e.g. google/gemini-2.5-flash-lite or its current successor; reasoning OFF; temperature 0): ONE call per item with up to 20 numbered situations; the prompt shows ONLY the sentence and the situations (never a formula) and asks, for each situation, TRUE / FALSE / UNDETERMINED ('the situation does not say enough to decide'), returning strict JSON {\\\"1\\\":\\\"TRUE\\\",...}; parse, retry once on invalid JSON, else mark that item's LLM judge a coverage failure\\n   ROBUSTNESS LLM: a second family (e.g. qwen/qwen3-30b-a3b-instruct or meta-llama/llama-3.3-70b-instruct) on a 300-item sha1 subsample of track L\\n   BATCHING CHECK: 100 items re-judged one world per call; report the agreement with batched verdicts\\nscores per item (for each judge j ∈ {nli_M1, nli_M2, llm, llm2}):\\n   n_worlds; determined = worlds judged TRUE/FALSE\\n   b2_mismatch_j = (# determined worlds with judge truth != φ truth) / max(1, #determined)\\n   b2_undet_j = (# UNDETERMINED) / n_worlds   # pre-registered DROP/coverage signal\\n   b2_score_j = b2_mismatch_j * (1 - b2_undet_j) + 0.5 * b2_undet_j   # pre-registered combination: undetermined counts half\\n   also per-direction mismatch (W+ vs W-) and per-operator mismatch, used as the error-type readout: the operator whose worlds disagree most = predicted type\\ncoverage_status: 'ok' | 'unparseable' | 'no_mutants' | 'no_world' (every world build failed or timed out) | 'too_big' | 'judge_fail'; for AUROC, every non-ok item gets the MAX error score (flagged), and coverage is also reported separately\\nGATE (world-judging accuracy): run the same B2 pipeline with the REFERENCE formula ρ in place of φ, on track-H corrected gold (302) + the distinct track-L references; truth of the sentence in each world := ρ's truth (exact); accuracy_j = fraction of worlds where the judge answers exactly that truth (UNDETERMINED counts as wrong; its rate is reported separately); table by words stratum × judge × operator × direction; target >= 0.85; if NLI accuracy < 0.70 on >=20-word sentences, record it and declare b2_score_llm the PRIMARY B2 score (this rule is written in prereg.json)\\n\\n# ===== 5. SECONDARY TRACKS =====\\nSYNTH (for the C3 MIX term; never pooled with real-error AUROC): for each track-H corrected gold formula and each census operator applicable (NEG, REV, QUANT, RESTR, CONN, DROP, SWAP, SCOPE, ADD-of-a-foreign-atom drawn from another item of the same story), one sha1-chosen perturbation that is z3-non-equivalent to the reference; score B1 and B2(NLI + LLM); per-operator synthetic sensitivity at the pre-registered threshold\\nDISGUISE (contamination): disguise(text, fol) = sha1-seeded consistent map from each content lemma (spaCy lemma, POS NOUN/VERB/ADJ/PROPN) that matches a predicate/constant token (by lemma or WordNet derivation) to a pronounceable nonce word (CVCV+CVC from a fixed syllable table); replace all inflections in the text and the matching token inside the predicate/constant names; function words, quantifier words, 'not/if/unless' untouched; assert that the mapping is consistent on all items (a text word maps to one nonce everywhere); run B1 (M1, M3) and B2 (NLI M1 + LLM) on disguised track-L + track-H; contamination delta = AUROC(orig) - AUROC(disguised), paired bootstrap; expect M3 (FOLIO-trained) to show the largest delta\\nINVARIANCE: score B1/B2 on the 150×5 rewrites; FA_family = fraction flagged at the pre-registered threshold; also flip_rate = flagged(rewrite) & not flagged(original)\\nLOCAL BASELINES on identical items (reuse iter-3 code): L1 lint (any smell → 1), bag-of-words content accounting (content_accounting.py score), parse_ok, and formula length (#atoms) as a trivial control\\n\\n# ===== 6. ANALYSIS (analysis.py) =====\\nprimary set = track-L items with label ∈ {CORRECT, ERROR} (UNCERTAIN, UNPARSEABLE and TIMEOUT reported separately; UNPARSEABLE also as coverage)\\nfor each score: AUROC, AUPRC (+ prevalence), precision/recall/FA at the prereg threshold, precision re-weighted to 10% and 25% prevalence; paired bootstrap 2,000 resamples clustered by norm(text), percentile 95% CI; ΔAUROC vs each local baseline and B2 vs B1, paired\\ntie-aware pairwise accuracy (Deutsch et al. 2023 acc_eq, with tie calibration epsilon chosen on track H) for the quantised B2 scores and the binary L1\\ncross-fitted fusion: logistic regression on [B1 features + B2 features]. SENTENCE-LEAK GUARD: the 202 FOLIO conclusions are the SAME sentences in track H and track L, so the primary model is fitted on track H with every sentence whose norm(text) occurs in track L REMOVED (in practice MALLS-test + non-overlapping items; log the n) and then applied to track L. The secondary model is within-track-L GroupKFold(5) by sentence. The same guard applies to prereg thresholds: calibrate on track-H CORRECT items whose sentence is not in track L (fall back to all track-H CORRECT only if n < 60, and log that); ΔAUROC of [L1 + bag-of-words + B] over [L1 + bag-of-words] (complementarity to FOL-Triage's cheap layers)\\nper-census-type sensitivity on track H (repair_census classes: ADD, DROP, CONN, UNGLUE, NEG, MOVE, SWAP, COMPOUND, ...) at the prereg threshold, with Wilson CIs; the same on track-L ERROR items by repair op; b1_t2f vs b1_f2t sensitivity split by DROP vs ADD (direction hypothesis)\\nerror-type readout: for single-op track-L/H errors, agreement between B2's argmax-disagreeing operator and the census operator (confusion matrix)\\npaired AUROC on the 99 track-H pairs (original vs corrected, same text) for comparison with iter-3's 0.59 bag-of-words\\ncomplexity: AUROC/FA by words stratum, n_quant, depth, n_conditions, exception; logistic slope of the score's discrimination vs log words (descriptive); a stratum is TESTED only if >=50 ERROR and >=50 CORRECT\\nper-system (gpt-3.5, gpt-4, davinci) AUROC and FA (descriptive if <50/50)\\nC3 inputs: per-operator synthetic sensitivity (SYNTH) vs real sensitivity (track H by census class), census-reweighted synthetic sensitivity vs observed real sensitivity\\ncost: OpenRouter $ per item (ledger), NLI GPU-seconds per item × A4500 $/h (log the rate used), z3 CPU-seconds per item; cost gate $0.002/item\\n\\n# ===== 7. OUTPUTS =====\\nmethod_out.json: {meta: {prereg_sha, match_rate, counts by track/label/system, budget_spent}, items: [{item_id, track, system, label, label_fine, strata, b1_min, b1_t2f, b1_f2t, b1_contra, b1_m2_*, b1_m3_*, b2_mismatch_nli, b2_undet_nli, b2_score_nli, b2_mismatch_llm, b2_undet_llm, b2_score_llm, b2_llm2_* (subset), b2_pred_op, n_worlds, n_mutants, coverage_status, cost_usd, seconds, disguised_{...}}], analysis: {auroc_table, fusion, per_type, invariance, gate_table, contamination, complexity, cost, synth_vs_real}} ; validate with aii-json (exp_gen_sol_out schema if applicable) and generate full/mini/preview; split with aii-file-size-limit if > limit\\nalso out/worlds_sample.jsonl (100 verbalised worlds with judge verdicts, for human inspection), data/screen_items.json, prereg.json, README.md (layout, how to run, reusable API docstrings), .aii/manifest.yaml (hf_cache/ and .venv/ -> delete redownloadable; out/ and data/ -> keep)\\nPUBLIC API (src/b_metrics.py, each with a precise docstring of what it measures): verbalize(fol)->str; b1_score(text, fol)->{t2f,f2t,min,contra}; build_worlds(fol, k=10)->list[{facts, phi_truth, op, direction}]; verbalize_world(facts)->str; b2_score(text, fol, judge='nli'|'llm')->{mismatch, undet, score, pred_op, n_worlds, coverage_status}; disguise(text, fol)->(text', fol')\",", "   \"fallback_plan\": \"F1 LOGIC-LM FILES: if the raw GitHub URL fails, clone the repo shallowly or use the GitHub API contents endpoint. If the record count is far below 204 (one web fetch reported 63; verify), use what exists, log it, and rely on the premise extension of the shared screen; do NOT pull other systems' outputs into track L (that would break byte-identity with the siblings). F2 LOW MATCH RATE (<150 distinct conclusions): apply the strategy's premise extension exactly as written. If track L still has fewer than 50 ERROR items, report the track-L AUROC as underpowered, and make track H plus the pooled L+H the descriptive headline while keeping track L as the pre-registered primary. F3 NLI FAILS THE WORLD GATE (<0.70 on >=20-word sentences or overall): per prereg, switch the primary B2 score to the LLM judge. If the LLM also scores <0.85 overall, report B2 as a negative (text-only judges cannot read templated worlds reliably), with the gate table broken down by operator/direction/stratum. Try ONE pre-declared alternative world format on 100 gate items: a compact table-like listing ('Students: Bonnie. Not students: thing1.'). Adopt it only if it improves gate accuracy by >=5 points, and log the switch as a post-hoc deviation. F4 B1 COLLAPSES ON TERSE PREDICATES (f2t near 0 for CORRECT items because 'Bonnie performs' does not entail 'Bonnie performs in school talent shows often'): this is the predicted failure. Report the B1-gloss diagnostic to quantify it, keep t2f (the DROP detector) as the deployable B1 score if f2t is uninformative, and state that B1 is vocabulary-sensitive. F5 z3 WORLD BUILDING SLOW OR FAILING: reduce K to 6 mutants, cap the domain at 3 plus constants, lower the Optimize timeout to 1 s, and fall back to Solver() without minimisation. Run a ProcessPoolExecutor with spawn and 6 workers. If coverage drops below 90%, report it: the coverage gate is part of the result, not something to engineer around. F6 GPU UNAVAILABLE: run M2 (base) for everything on CPU, and M1 only for B1 and the gate subset, logging the substitution. F7 BUDGET: the pre-sweep estimate must be <= $4 (expected ≈ $1.5: ~3.5k batched calls × ~1.8k input tokens at ~$0.10/M plus output). Check the ledger after every call. At $6 stop the robustness LLM and the batching check; at $9 stop all LLM calls and report NLI-only B2 for the remaining items with coverage_status 'judge_fail'. F8 LABELLER TIMEOUTS: items whose label search exceeds 25 s become UNCERTAIN(COMPOUND) exactly as in repair_census.search; equivalence timeouts become TIMEOUT_UNKNOWN; neither is merged with CORRECT. F9 DISGUISE BREAKS SENTENCES: if more than 20% of items have no text-predicate lemma match (nothing to disguise), report contamination only on the items with at least 2 mapped lemmas, with that n.\",", "   \"testing_plan\": \"T0 UNIT TESTS (must pass before any sweep; pytest in tests/): (a) parser precedence: 'A(x) ⊕ B(x) → C(x)' parses as imp(xor(A,B),C), '∀x (A(x)) ∧ ∀y (B(y))' parses as two conjoined sentences, and the 302 curated references parse at >= the iter-3 rate (8 known failures); (b) labeller regression: re-running the census labeller on the 99 track-H pairs reproduces iter-3 repair_census.rows.json classes except the precedence-affected items (expect about 3 COMPOUND->repaired; list every diff); (c) verbalize on 20 hand-written formulas including ∃x(A(x)→B(x)), ∀x(A(x)∧B(x)), ∀x(A(x)∧B(x)↔C(x)), xor, nested quantifiers, 0-ary and 3-ary atoms: assert the literal non-repaired forms and that no two non-identical ASTs give the same string (injectivity check over the 302 references); (d) worlds: for 50 random (φ,ψ) pairs, the Python finite-model evaluator agrees with the intended φ truth in every returned world, and φ≠ψ truth holds in each; (e) NLI sanity: 3 known pairs per model ('A man sleeps.'/'A person sleeps.' entail; '... is awake' contradiction) come out with the right labels via config.id2label; (f) disguise: the round trip keeps FOL parseable and z3-equivalent under renaming; the text keeps its logic words. T1 MINI RUN (30 track-L + 20 track-H items, sha1-first): end-to-end B1+B2 with NLI and LLM. Inspect 20 verbalisations and 20 verbalised worlds by printing them to logs; measure seconds/item and $/item, then extrapolate to the full run (must fit <= 2.5 h wall-clock and <= $4) before scaling. T2 GATE PILOT (100 reference worlds, stratified by words): compute judge accuracy for NLI-M1 and the LLM. It is a go signal if the LLM is >= 0.75 on the pilot. If both are < 0.6, stop scaling B2 and move to F3 early, so the time budget goes to B1 and the analysis. T3 SCALE 200 -> FULL per aii-long-running-tasks, running in the background with PID tracking and time logged per stage. T4 CONFIRMATION SIGNALS before the analysis is trusted: the label-vector sha256 is logged and stable across two builds; CORRECT items' b1_min distribution is shifted higher than ERROR items' on track H (a sanity check, not selection); invariance FA for RENAME/REORDER are near 0 for B2 (it is truth-functional, so REORDER/DEMORGAN/PRENEX/CONTRAPOS should give identical worlds up to form; any FA there is a bug to investigate before reporting); every coverage failure is counted in method_out.json. T5 OUTPUT CHECKS: method_out.json validates with aii-json; mini/preview files are generated; the README documents the layout and restore commands; .aii/manifest.yaml covers hf_cache/ and .venv/ (delete, redownloadable) and keeps out/, data/, prereg.json.\"", " }"]}], "userModified": false, "replaceAll": false}

(1) BASELINES every reference-free faithfulness paper reports: an LLM judge (cheap and strong), NLI-based entailment scores (SummaC/TRUE-style: NLI between source and output, taken in both directions for coverage), a QA/probe-based metric, round-trip similarity, and trivial baselines (length, parse/compile rate). For formal outputs the field adds solver checks such as round-trip equivalence (2604.25031) and sampling consensus (2410.20936). The first comparison a reviewer names for an NLI-on-verbalisation metric is round-trip with an LLM verbaliser (sibling D computes it) and the plain LLM judge. The fair-tuning convention is one fixed prompt per model with no tuning on the evaluation labels, and thresholds fixed on a separate calibration set. (3) CONTROLS: the same items and labels for every metric (paired design); the NLI checkpoint and prompt fixed before labels are seen; contamination checked with disguised or renamed items; invariance checked with meaning-preserving rewrites. (4) HOW MUCH IS ENOUGH: TRUE/SummaC-style meta-evaluations report ROC AUC on a few hundred to a few thousand binary-labelled items, with bootstrap CIs. Below about 50 positives and 50 negatives per cell nobody reads a per-stratum AUROC as more than descriptive. With about 150 errors and 350 correct items, the AUROC SE is about 0.025 and the minimum detectable paired ΔAUROC is about 0.06-0.07. (5) MEASURES: item-level ROC AUC (threshold-free) is the primary measure, with AUPRC always stated alongside its prevalence. Precision/recall/false-alarm rate are reported at a threshold fixed in advance. Binary or quantised scores are compared with tie-aware pairwise accuracy (Deutsch et al. 2023). Uncertainty comes from a paired bootstrap resampled by sentence cluster. STRICT rewrites (seed 0): RENAME (each predicate -> WordNet synonym of its head token of equal arity, or an '_x' suffix if none; constants -> case/underscore variant), REORDER (swap operands of every ∧/∨), DEMORGAN (push one ¬ through ∧/∨ or introduce ¬¬-free De Morgan at the top connective), PRENEX (pull quantifiers out where no capture occurs), CONTRAPOS (A→B => ¬B→¬A at the top implication); z3-verify each non-rename rewrite as equivalent to the original; drop and count failures\\nwrite data/screen_items.json [{item_id, track, system, text, candidate_fol, reference_fol, ref_type, label, label_fine, repair_ops, ambiguous, strata, story_id}] + sha256 of the label vector in the log\\n\\n# ===== 2. PRE-REGISTRATION (before any score is joined to track-L labels) =====\\nprereg.json = {timestamp, sha256 of src/*.py, verbalizer templates, NLI model ids+revisions, LLM model id + exact prompt, mutant operator list + sampling rule, world limits, score definitions + orientation (higher = more likely error), operating thresholds = the 90th percentile of each score on track-H items labelled CORRECT whose sentence does not occur in track L (known-good human gold; NOT track L; see the leak guard in step 6), selection-relevant analyses, stratum testability rule (>=50 ERROR and >=50 CORRECT), bootstrap settings}\\n\\n# ===== 3. B1: template verbaliser + bidirectional NLI =====\\ndef pred_phrase(name, args):  # deterministic, non-repairing\\n   toks = split camelCase/snake/digits -> lower\\n   head = toks[0]\\n   if head is a verb per WordNet (any verb synset) and not (noun-first and no verb morphology) and not ending in -ed/-ing: VERB -> '{a0} ' + lemminflect 3sg(head) + rest + (' ' + a1 ... joined with ' and ' for arity>2)\\n   elif head endswith ('ed','ing') or WordNet adj/participle: '{a0} is ' + ' '.join(toks) + (' ' + a1)\\n   else NOUN: unary '{a0} is a/an ' + phrase ; binary '{a0} is ' + phrase + ' of ' + a1 ; arity>=3 '{a0}, {a1} and {a2} stand in the ' + phrase + ' relation'\\n   args: variables stay as 'x','y'; constants -> split camel/underscore, Title Case\\n   arity 0: 'it is the case that ' + phrase\\ndef verbalize(e):\\n   all v B  -> 'for every thing ' + v + ', ' + verbalize(B)\\n   ex v B   -> 'there is some thing ' + v + ' such that ' + verbalize(B)\\n   not atom -> atom phrase with 'is not'/'does not' ; not complex -> 'it is not the case that (' + ... + ')'\\n   imp -> 'if ' + A + ', then ' + B ; iff -> A + ' if and only if ' + B ; xor -> 'either ' + A + ' or ' + B + ', but not both' ; or -> A + ' or ' + B ; and -> A + ' and ' + B\\n   nested binaries parenthesised with commas only where a child is itself binary (to keep scope visible); NO simplification, NO restrictor repair: ∃x(A→B) -> 'there is some thing x such that if x is an A, then x is a B'\\n   capitalise the first letter, add '.'\\nNLI models: M1 = MoritzLaurer/DeBERTa-v3-large-mnli-fever-anli-ling-wanli (primary); M2 = cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-base (robustness, MNLI+SNLI, NOT FOLIO-trained); M3 = tasksource/deberta-base-long-nli (FOLIO-TRAINED: contamination probe only). Read label indices from model.config.id2label (never hard-code); fp16; batch 32 (halve on OOM); max_length 512 (M3: 1280); log the truncation count\\nb1_t2f = P_entail(premise=text, hyp=verb) ; b1_f2t = P_entail(premise=verb, hyp=text) ; b1_min = min ; b1_contra = max(P_contra both ways)\\noriented error scores: 1-b1_min, 1-b1_t2f (predicted DROP detector), 1-b1_f2t (predicted ADD detector), b1_contra\\nB1-gloss DIAGNOSTIC (track L only, clearly marked not-deployable): the same, but predicates rendered with Logic-LM's own 'Predicates:' gloss; it measures how much of B1's failure is predicate-name terseness\\n\\n# ===== 4. B2: solver-built distinguishing worlds judged against the TEXT =====\\nMUT_OPS = {NEG, REV, QUANT, RESTR, CONN, DROP, SWAP, SCOPE, MOVE, UNGLUE, BIND}  # ADD needs vocabulary outside φ; its role is played by DROP-mutant worlds (they vary an added conjunct) and by the UNDETERMINED signal\\ndef mutants(φ): cands = [(op,ψ) for op,ψ in edits(φ, φ) if op in MUT_OPS]; dedupe by str; drop ψ with fp(ψ)==fp(φ) and equivalent(ψ,φ,1500) is True (also drop None/timeouts and count them)\\n   order by sha1(item_id|op|str(ψ)); round-robin over operator types; keep K<=10 (at least 6 when available)\\ndef world(φ, ψ, want_phi_true):\\n   consts = constants of φ∪ψ ; for k_extra in 0..3 (total <= max(4, len(consts)+1), hard cap 6):\\n      D = z3.EnumSort('D', consts_names + ['thing1'..'thing{k}'])   # constants = distinct named elements (unique-names assumption)\\n      translate φ, ψ with a copy of fol.to_z3 parameterised by sort D (atoms over constants map to their enum element)\\n      goal = And(φ, Not(ψ)) if want_phi_true else And(Not(φ), ψ)\\n      opt = z3.Optimize(); opt.set(timeout=2000); opt.add(goal); opt.minimize(Sum(If(atom,1,0) for every ground atom))  # the smallest, most readable world\\n      if sat: return facts = {ground atom: bool}   # fall back to Solver() when Optimize is unknown\\n   return None\\nfor each (op,ψ): W+ = world(φ,ψ,True) (φ TRUE there), W- = world(φ,ψ,False) (φ FALSE); dedupe worlds by fact set; verify each world by a pure-Python evaluator of φ (fol AST over the finite domain) and assert that truth == the expected value\\ndef verbalize_world(facts): 'There are exactly N individuals: Bonnie, thing1 and thing2.' + for each unary/0-ary predicate and each individual, one positive or negative fact sentence via pred_phrase ('Bonnie is a student.' / 'thing1 is not a student.'); for binary+: the positive facts, then 'No other <phrase> facts hold.'; cap 60 facts, and if exceeded mark the world 'too_big' and skip it\\nJUDGE-NLI: premise = situation, hypothesis = text -> argmax label: entailment=TRUE, contradiction=FALSE, neutral=UNDETERMINED; the prompt shows ONLY the sentence and the situations (never a formula) and asks, for each situation, TRUE / FALSE / UNDETERMINED ('the situation does not say enough to decide'), returning strict JSON {\\\"1\\\":\\\"TRUE\\\",...}; parse, retry once on invalid JSON, else mark that item's LLM judge a coverage failure\\n   ROBUSTNESS LLM: a second family (e.g. qwen/qwen3-30b-a3b-instruct or meta-llama/llama-3.3-70b-instruct) on a 300-item sha1 subsample of track L\\n   BATCHING CHECK: 100 items re-judged one world per call; report the agreement with batched verdicts\\nscores per item (for each judge j ∈ {nli_M1, nli_M2, llm, llm2}):\\n   n_worlds; determined = worlds judged TRUE/FALSE\\n   b2_mismatch_j = (# determined worlds with judge truth != φ truth) / max(1, #determined)\\n   b2_undet_j = (# UNDETERMINED) / n_worlds   # pre-registered DROP/coverage signal\\n   b2_score_j = b2_mismatch_j * (1 - b2_undet_j) + 0.5 * b2_undet_j   # pre-registered combination: undetermined counts half\\n   also per-direction mismatch (W+ vs W-) and per-operator mismatch, used as the error-type readout: the operator whose worlds disagree most = predicted type\\ncoverage_status: 'ok' | 'unparseable' | 'no_mutants' | 'no_world' (every world build failed or timed out) | 'too_big' | 'judge_fail'; for AUROC, every non-ok item gets the MAX error score (flagged), and coverage is also reported separately\\nGATE (world-judging accuracy): run the same B2 pipeline with the REFERENCE formula ρ in place of φ, on track-H corrected gold (302) + the distinct track-L references; truth of the sentence in each world := ρ's truth (exact); accuracy_j = fraction of worlds where the judge answers exactly that truth (UNDETERMINED counts as wrong; its rate is reported separately); table by words stratum × judge × operator × direction; target >= 0.85; if NLI accuracy < 0.70 on >=20-word sentences, record it and declare b2_score_llm the PRIMARY B2 score (this rule is written in prereg.json)\\n\\n# ===== 5. SECONDARY TRACKS =====\\nSYNTH (for the C3 MIX term; never pooled with real-error AUROC): for each track-H corrected gold formula and each census operator applicable (NEG, REV, QUANT, RESTR, CONN, DROP, SWAP, SCOPE, ADD-of-a-foreign-atom drawn from another item of the same story), one sha1-chosen perturbation that is z3-non-equivalent to the reference; score B1 and B2(NLI + LLM); per-operator synthetic sensitivity at the pre-registered threshold\\nDISGUISE (contamination): disguise(text, fol) = sha1-seeded consistent map from each content lemma (spaCy lemma, POS NOUN/VERB/ADJ/PROPN) that matches a predicate/constant token (by lemma or WordNet derivation) to a pronounceable nonce word (CVCV+CVC from a fixed syllable table); replace all inflections in the text and the matching token inside the predicate/constant names; function words, quantifier words, 'not/if/unless' untouched; assert that the mapping is consistent on all items (a text word maps to one nonce everywhere); run B1 (M1, M3) and B2 (NLI M1 + LLM) on disguised track-L + track-H; contamination delta = AUROC(orig) - AUROC(disguised), paired bootstrap; expect M3 (FOLIO-trained) to show the largest delta\\nINVARIANCE: score B1/B2 on the 150×5 rewrites; FA_family = fraction flagged at the pre-registered threshold; also flip_rate = flagged(rewrite) & not flagged(original)\\nLOCAL BASELINES on identical items (reuse iter-3 code): L1 lint (any smell → 1), bag-of-words content accounting (content_accounting.py score), parse_ok, and formula length (#atoms) as a trivial control\\n\\n# ===== 6. ANALYSIS (analysis.py) =====\\nprimary set = track-L items with label ∈ {CORRECT, ERROR} (UNCERTAIN, UNPARSEABLE and TIMEOUT reported separately; UNPARSEABLE also as coverage)\\nfor each score: AUROC, AUPRC (+ prevalence), precision/recall/FA at the prereg threshold, precision re-weighted to 10% and 25% prevalence; paired bootstrap 2,000 resamples clustered by norm(text), percentile 95% CI; ΔAUROC vs each local baseline and B2 vs B1, paired\\ntie-aware pairwise accuracy (Deutsch et al. 2023 acc_eq, with tie calibration epsilon chosen on track H) for the quantised B2 scores and the binary L1\\ncross-fitted fusion: logistic regression on [B1 features + B2 features]. SENTENCE-LEAK GUARD: the 202 FOLIO conclusions are the SAME sentences in track H and track L, so the primary model is fitted on track H with every sentence whose norm(text) occurs in track L REMOVED (in practice MALLS-test + non-overlapping items; log the n) and then applied to track L. The secondary model is within-track-L GroupKFold(5) by sentence. The same guard applies to prereg thresholds: calibrate on track-H CORRECT items whose sentence is not in track L (fall back to all track-H CORRECT only if n < 60, and log that); ΔAUROC of [L1 + bag-of-words + B] over [L1 + bag-of-words] (complementarity to FOL-Triage's cheap layers)\\nper-census-type sensitivity on track H (repair_census classes: ADD, DROP, CONN, UNGLUE, NEG, MOVE, SWAP, COMPOUND, ...) at the prereg threshold, with Wilson CIs; the same on track-L ERROR items by repair op; b1_t2f vs b1_f2t sensitivity split by DROP vs ADD (direction hypothesis)\\nerror-type readout: for single-op track-L/H errors, agreement between B2's argmax-disagreeing operator and the census operator (confusion matrix)\\npaired AUROC on the 99 track-H pairs (original vs corrected, same text) for comparison with iter-3's 0.59 bag-of-words\\ncomplexity: AUROC/FA by words stratum, n_quant, depth, n_conditions, exception; logistic slope of the score's discrimination vs log words (descriptive); a stratum is TESTED only if >=50 ERROR and >=50 CORRECT\\nper-system (gpt-3.5, gpt-4, davinci) AUROC and FA (descriptive if <50/50)\\nC3 inputs: per-operator synthetic sensitivity (SYNTH) vs real sensitivity (track H by census class), census-reweighted synthetic sensitivity vs observed real sensitivity\\ncost: OpenRouter $ per item (ledger), NLI GPU-seconds per item × A4500 $/h (log the rate used), z3 CPU-seconds per item; cost gate $0.002/item\\n\\n# ===== 7. OUTPUTS =====\\nmethod_out.json: {meta: {prereg_sha, match_rate, counts by track/label/system, budget_spent}, items: [{item_id, track, system, label, label_fine, strata, b1_min, b1_t2f, b1_f2t, b1_contra, b1_m2_*, b1_m3_*, b2_mismatch_nli, b2_undet_nli, b2_score_nli, b2_mismatch_llm, b2_undet_llm, b2_score_llm, b2_llm2_* (subset), b2_pred_op, n_worlds, n_mutants, coverage_status, cost_usd, seconds, disguised_{...}}], analysis: {auroc_table, fusion, per_type, invariance, gate_table, contamination, complexity, cost, synth_vs_real}} ; validate with aii-json (exp_gen_sol_out schema if applicable) and generate full/mini/preview; split with aii-file-size-limit if > limit\\nalso out/worlds_sample.jsonl (100 verbalised worlds with judge verdicts, for human inspection), data/screen_items.json, prereg.json, README.md (layout, how to run, reusable API docstrings), .aii/manifest.yaml (hf_cache/ and .venv/ -> delete redownloadable; out/ and data/ -> keep)\\nPUBLIC API (src/b_metrics.py, each with a precise docstring of what it measures): verbalize(fol)->str; b1_score(text, fol)->{t2f,f2t,min,contra}; build_worlds(fol, k=10)->list[{facts, phi_truth, op, direction}]; verbalize_world(facts)->str; b2_score(text, fol, judge='nli'|'llm')->{mismatch, undet, score, pred_op, n_worlds, coverage_status}; disguise(text, fol)->(text', fol')\",", "   \"fallback_plan\": \"F1 LOGIC-LM FILES: if the raw GitHub URL fails, clone the repo shallowly or use the GitHub API contents endpoint. # GEN_PLAN: experiment_iter1_dir2 (verbalise-and-entail screen)

This is a planning step only. No code was run and no data was downloaded.

## What this step did
It wrote the executor plan for screening two gold-free NL→FOL faithfulness candidates on the run's shared frozen screen:
- **B1** renders the formula as literal, non-repairing template English and checks it against the source sentence with bidirectional NLI.
- **B2** has z3 build small worlds that tell the candidate apart from its own typed single-edit mutants. A text-only judge (NLI or a cheap LLM) then says whether the sentence is TRUE, FALSE or UNDETERMINED in each world.

## Layout
- `.terminal_claude_agent_struct_out.json`: the plan. Fields: title, summary, compute profile, domain_practice, practice_alignment, builds_on, implementation_pseudocode, fallback_plan, testing_plan.
- `.aii/manifest.yaml`: heavy-path decisions. There are none; this workspace holds only text.
- `README.md`: this file.

## How to use
The executor reads the plan JSON. The inputs it names are read-only paths under
`/ai-inventor/aii_data/runs/run_u75jRHUss0zo/iter_3/gen_hypo/claude_agent/{data,probes}`.

## Restoring removed files
None. No files are marked `delete`.

File: /root/.claude/projects/-ai-inventor/memory/project_nl2fol_faithfulness_genplan_exp2_iter1_run_u75jRHUss0zo.md

---
name: project-nl2fol-faithfulness-genplan-exp2-iter1-run-u75jrhuss0zo
description: run_u75jRHUss0zo invention-loop iter1 GEN_PLAN experiment_2 — B1 template-verbaliser bidirectional NLI + B2 z3 distinguishing worlds judged text-only (TRUE/FALSE/UNDETERMINED); UNDET rate = DROP signal; tasksource NLI is FOLIO-trained
metadata:
  type: project
---
Plan for the verbalise-and-entail screen (Exp B), written 2026-09-23. It follows [[project_nl2fol_faithfulness_genstrat_iter1_run_u75jRHUss0zo]], and its screen and label choices match those of [[project-nl2fol-faithfulness-genplan-exp1-iter1-run-u75jrhuss0zo]].

**Key design choices**
- B2 worlds contain only the candidate's own predicates. A condition the candidate DROPPED therefore leaves the sentence UNDETERMINED, and b2_undet is pre-registered as the coverage signal.
- ADDED predicates show up through the worlds of DROP-mutants: they vary the extra conjunct while the truth of the text stays fixed.
- Worlds are built as z3 EnumSort finite domains. Constants are distinct elements (unique-names assumption). z3 Optimize minimises the number of true atoms.
- The LLM judge makes one batched call per item, with up to 20 situations; the estimate is about $1.5 in total.

**Facts found while planning**
- The tasksource/deberta-*-nli models were TRAINED on FOLIO, FOL-nli and LogicNLI, so they are contaminated for a FOLIO screen and serve only as a contamination probe.
  - Primary NLI model: MoritzLaurer/DeBERTa-v3-large-mnli-fever-anli-ling-wanli.
  - Robustness model: cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-base.
- Logic-LM programs include a 'Predicates:' section with English glosses. The plan ignores it for the metric and uses it only for a B1-gloss diagnostic of predicate-name terseness.
- Closest prior art for B2 is SSV (Raza & Milic-Frayling, IJCAI 2025, arXiv 2501.16961), which runs in the reverse direction: the LLM makes instances and the solver checks them.

### [77] ASSISTANT · 2026-09-23 13:01:29 UTC

```
I've written the plan for the verbalise-and-entail screen to `./.terminal_claude_agent_struct_out.json`. I couldn't run a JSON parser here (no shell), so I checked the escaping by pattern search instead.

The plan asks for the `gpu_basic` profile. It rebuilds the shared screen exactly as the strategy defines it: the same local files, the same labeller with the ⊕/→ precedence bug in `fol.py` fixed, and the same `item_id` scheme. That lets iteration 2 join its scores with the three sibling experiments.

- **B1 (read the formula back as English):** a fixed-template verbaliser that never tidies the formula up, e.g. ∃x(A→B) comes out as "there is some thing x such that if…". It then checks the sentence against that English with an NLI model in both directions. The main model is `MoritzLaurer/DeBERTa-v3-large-mnli-fever-anli-ling-wanli`, with `cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-base` as a robustness check. One direction (sentence → verbalisation) is expected to catch dropped conditions, the other to catch added ones.
- **B2 (test worlds):** z3 makes small single-edit variants of the formula, then builds tiny worlds (up to 4 individuals, fewest true facts) where the formula and each variant disagree. A judge that sees only the sentence says TRUE, FALSE or UNDETERMINED for each world: the NLI model first, then a cheap OpenRouter model with one call per item.
  - The first score is how often the judge disagrees with the formula's own truth value.
  - The second is how often the judge answers UNDETERMINED. The worlds only mention the formula's own predicates, so a condition the formula dropped leaves the sentence undecidable. This is fixed in advance as the dropped-condition signal.
- **Quality gate:** the judges are first tested on worlds built from the trusted reference formulas, where the right answer is known exactly. The target is ≥85% accuracy, reported by sentence length. If NLI falls below 70% on sentences of 20+ words, the LLM judge becomes the main B2 score.
- **Budget:** about $1.5 of OpenRouter spend is expected, with a planned ceiling of $4 and a hard stop at $9.

Things I found while planning that shaped it:
- **The tasksource NLI models were trained on FOLIO**, so they can't be trusted on this screen. The plan uses them only as a contamination probe.
- **Closest prior work is SSV** (Raza & Milic-Frayling, IJCAI 2025, arXiv 2501.16961). It works the other way round: the LLM makes the examples and the solver checks them. The plan cites it rather than claiming the idea as new.
- **Sentence leakage:** the same 202 FOLIO conclusions sit in both the human-error track and the LLM-output track. The plan therefore fits the combined model and sets the alarm thresholds only on human-track items whose sentences don't appear in the LLM track. This matches the sibling FOL-Triage plan, and so do the label mapping and computing strata from the reference formula.
- **Automatic labels may be noisy.** A parallel run on the same corpora found that about 44% of candidates the solver called non-equivalent were judged faithful by a 3-model panel. The plan keeps a fine-grained label on every item so it can be re-scored once the panel-adjudicated labels arrive.
- **The Logic-LM output count is unconfirmed.** One fetch reported only 63 records instead of about 204, possibly truncation. The executor is told to log the real count and, if needed, fall back to the premise extension the strategy defines.

I also wrote a `README.md` and an empty `.aii/manifest.yaml` (the workspace holds only text), and saved a memory note for later rounds.
```
